*Mengchu Xu,Yonina C. Eldar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对稀疏相位恢复中的谱初始化问题，提出了对角支撑筛选的阶段特异性设计理论。通过将每个坐标得分建模为区分支撑/非支撑坐标的标量统计量，采用偏转准则量化筛选质量，在希尔伯特空间框架下刻画了最优谱预处理器。在高斯模型下唯一最优解为中心化线性预处理器；针对有界实现引入球面归一化并刻画精确最优解，进一步为克服边界奇异性构造了有界替代预处理器并证明其最优性。建立了有限样本对角桥连接筛选商与初始化样本复杂度，数值实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谱初始化设计理论主要针对稠密相位恢复（目标为特征向量估计），而稀疏初始化需要统计上截然不同的支撑筛选步骤，其设计理论尚不清晰。

Method: 将坐标得分视为区分支撑/非支撑的标量统计量，采用偏转准则衡量筛选质量；在希尔伯特空间框架下刻画最优谱预处理器；高斯模型下推导最优解；引入球面归一化处理有界性约束；构造有界替代预处理器并证明最优性；建立有限样本对角桥连接理论商与样本复杂度。

Result: 高斯模型下唯一最优谱预处理器为中心化线性预处理器；球面归一化精确最优解存在边界奇异性；构造的有界替代预处理器（高斯规则的仅方向投影）在替代偏转准则下唯一最优；有限样本对角桥表明筛选商可预测初始化样本复杂度，经验估计信号能量仅引入低阶扰动。

Conclusion: 数值实验显示高斯中心化规则与球面对应在筛选和初始化层面几乎一致，验证了设计商预测的排序，为稀疏相位恢复的谱初始化提供了系统的阶段特异性设计理论。

Abstract: Spectral initialization is a critical yet challenging step in sparse phase retrieval. Existing spectral design theory is largely tailored to dense phase retrieval, where the objective is eigenvector estimation. In contrast, sparse initialization first requires a statistically distinct support screening step whose design remains much less understood. This paper develops a stage-specific design theory for diagonal support screening. We formulate each coordinate score as a scalar statistic for distinguishing support from non-support coordinates and adopt the deflection criterion as a tractable measure of screening quality. Within a Hilbert-space formulation, we characterize the optimal spectral preprocessors that maximize this criterion. In the Gaussian model, the unique optimum is the centered linear preprocessor. To obtain a bounded implementation, we introduce a spherical normalization and characterize its exact optimal preprocessor. Since the exact spherical optimum exhibits a boundary singularity, we construct a bounded surrogate preprocessor and establish its unique optimality under a surrogate deflection criterion. The surrogate optimum is shown to be the direction-only projection of the Gaussian rule, removing the unbounded radial factor while preserving the same first-order screening structure. We further establish a general finite-sample diagonal bridge that connects the exact and surrogate deflection quotients to the initialization sample complexity, and that replacing the unknown signal energy by its empirical estimate introduces only a lower-order perturbation. Numerical experiments are consistent with the ordering predicted by the design quotients and show that the Gaussian centered rule and its spherical counterpart behave nearly identically at both the screening and initialization levels.

</details>


### [79] [Differential LEO Navigation under Asynchronous Satellite Clocks: Architecture and Performance Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21621)
*Qamar Bader,Sharief Saleh,Henk Wymeersch,Gonzalo Seco-Granados,Aboelmagd Noureldin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基站辅助的差分导航架构，通过基站连续跟踪LEO卫星时钟偏差并作为测量域校正传递给流动站，有效消除独立时钟误差引起的统计不一致性，且无需增大流动站状态向量。基于真实城市车辆数据与高保真LEO星座仿真验证，该方法使后验估计不确定度精确匹配理论RBCRB界限，在几何退化条件下仍能提供高可靠的定位、导航与授时服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨通信卫星虽可作为GNSS拒止环境下的机会信号源，但其星座缺乏全球同步，各卫星独立的时钟偏差与漂移会严重恶化运动估计并引起统计不一致性，现有方法未能有效解决这一问题。

Method: 提出基站辅助差分导航架构：固定基站连续跟踪时变卫星时钟状态，将测量域校正及其严格传播的不确定度传递给流动站的8状态扩展卡尔曼滤波器(EKF)；引入鲁棒的可见性管理机制处理卫星频繁进出；推导与信道域信号模型直接关联的递归贝叶斯克拉美-罗界(RBCRB)作为理论性能极限。

Result: 基于真实城市车辆数据与高保真LEO星座仿真验证：差分框架完全消除了单接收机基线中固有的统计不一致性；流动站的后验估计不确定度在所有运动学和时钟状态上精确跟踪理论RBCRB界限；在几何严重退化期间仍保证高度可靠的PNT。

Conclusion: 所提差分架构有效解决了LEO卫星非同步时钟导致的统计不一致问题，在不增大流动站状态向量的前提下实现了接近理论极限的定位性能，为GNSS受限环境下的弹性PNT提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Low Earth Orbit (LEO) communication satellites have emerged as a promising source of signals of opportunity for resilient positioning, navigation, and timing (PNT) in GNSS-challenged environments. Unlike GNSS constellations, however, commercial LEO systems are not globally synchronized, and independent satellite clock biases and drifts severely degrade kinematic estimation and induce statistical inconsistency when not explicitly managed. This paper proposes a base-station-aided differential navigation architecture that explicitly mitigates independent LEO satellite clock biases and drifts without inflating the state vector of the mobile rover. A fixed base station continuously tracks time-varying per-satellite clock states and transmits them as measurement-domain corrections, along with their rigorously propagated uncertainties, to a compact, 8-state rover Extended Kalman Filter (EKF). To support this architecture, we introduce a robust visibility management scheme to seamlessly handle the frequent entry and exit of LEO satellites. Furthermore, we derive the Recursive Bayesian Cramer-Rao Bound (RBCRB) directly linked to the channel-domain signal model to establish fundamental theoretical performance limits. The proposed methodology is validated using real-world urban vehicular data combined with high-fidelity LEO constellation simulations. Results demonstrate that the differential framework completely eliminates the statistical inconsistency endemic to single-receiver baselines. Crucially, the rover's posterior estimation uncertainty perfectly tracks the theoretical RBCRB limits across all kinematic and clock states, ensuring highly reliable PNT even during periods of severe geometric degradation.

</details>


### [80] [Adaptive 5G Resource Allocation for Multistatic ISAC-Based UAV Detection and Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21677)
*Cole Dickerson,Wahab Khawaja,Ismail Guvenc*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应多基地ISAC框架，在5G网络中实现负载感知的无人机探测与跟踪，通过软件定义传感器利用外部机会信号缓解资源竞争，在动态负载下保持感知能力的同时降低跟踪中断概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在带来广泛应用的同时也对关键基础设施和受限空域构成安全风险，而ISAC虽可利用现有无线网络进行无人机监视，但实际部署中面临感知与通信的资源竞争以及低RCS高机动无人机的跟踪挑战。

Method: 建立共享资源框架量化感知波形长度、感知传输速率和波束分配对5G NR通信吞吐量的影响；利用ZC波形分析探测性能，采用M-of-N检测模型评估跟踪连续性；引入软件定义传感器节点在资源受限时利用外部机会信号提供补充被动感知。

Result: 自适应感知策略在动态负载下优于固定感知预留，能保持吞吐量并提供有用感知能力；严重拥塞时SDS辅助显著降低跟踪中断；CRLB分析表明多基地感知几何比单基地感知具有更好的定位精度和空间覆盖均匀性。

Conclusion: 协调自适应感知和分布式多基地支持是实现未来无线网络中弹性无人机监视的实用路径。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) enable numerous commercial and public-safety applications, yet they also create security risks near critical infrastructure, transportation hubs, and restricted airspace. While integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) can leverage existing wireless networks for UAV surveillance, practical deployment must address competition between sensing and communication demands, as well as the challenges associated with tracking highly maneuverable UAVs with low radar cross section (RCS). This paper investigates adaptive multistatic ISAC for load-aware UAV detection and tracking in 5G wireless networks. A shared-resource framework is developed to quantify how sensing waveform length, sensing transmission rate, and beam allocation affect communication throughput in a 5G new radio (NR) system. Detection performance is analyzed using Zadoff-Chu (ZC) sensing waveforms, while tracking continuity is evaluated through an M-of-N detection model. To improve robustness under congestion, software-defined sensor (SDS) nodes exploit external signals of opportunity (SoO) to provide supplemental passive sensing opportunities when network resources become limited. Results show that adaptive sensing policies outperform fixed sensing reservations by preserving throughput under dynamic load while maintaining useful sensing capability. Under heavy congestion, SDS assistance substantially reduces tracking outage in the simulated scenarios. Cramer-Rao lower bound (CRLB) analysis demonstrates that multistatic sensing geometries improve localization accuracy and provide more uniform spatial coverage than monostatic sensing alone. These results highlight coordinated adaptive sensing and distributed multistatic support as a practical path toward resilient UAV surveillance in future wireless networks.

</details>


### [81] [A Compact Cross-Structured Dynamic Antenna for Reconfigurable Directional Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21723)
*Sheng Huang,Jacob R. Randall,Cory Hilton,Jeffrey A. Nanzer*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种紧凑型交叉结构动态天线，通过可重构信息波束控制（而非传统辐射波束转向）实现天线级物理层安全。天线使用四个印刷曲折线单极子构成平面交叉结构，通过开关网络实现两种互补激励状态，产生二维相位中心位移，支持四个信息波束方向。实验证明在5.05 GHz下，16-QAM调制信号仅在预期E面信息波束扇区内保持低误码率，而离轴角度即使高信噪比也会出现不可恢复星座，实现了无需相控阵波束赋形、多RF链或机械运动的物理层安全传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理层安全方法依赖相控阵波束赋形、多RF链路或机械运动，复杂度高、成本大。本文旨在提出一种更简单的天线级方案，通过动态天线自身的信息波束控制实现物理层安全，无需复杂硬件支持。

Method: 设计了一种紧凑交叉结构动态天线，包含四个印刷曲折线单极子和开关网络，通过切换对向或对角端口组实现两种互补激励状态，产生二维相位中心位移。提出平均-差分阵列因子公式：平均分量保持全向覆盖，奇对称差分分量产生角度依赖的幅度和相位畸变，从而决定星座可恢复区域。

Result: 5.05 GHz原型在Rogers RO4350B上制造，电尺寸0.57×0.47λ₀²。16-QAM测量结果显示：低误码率仅局限于预期的E面信息波束扇区；离轴角度出现大幅幅度/相位误差、高BER或不可恢复星座；H面近全角度范围保持低BER，证实正交平面全向信息恢复。

Conclusion: 提出的动态天线通过信息波束控制成功实现了天线级物理层安全，无需相控阵波束赋形、多RF链或机械运动，在E面实现定向安全传输，H面保持全向覆盖，为低成本物理层安全提供新途径。

Abstract: A compact cross-structured dynamic antenna is presented for antenna-level physical-layer security using reconfigurable information-beam control rather than conventional radiation beam steering. The antenna uses four printed meander-line monopoles in a planar cross structure and a switching network that realizes two complementary excitation states for each dynamic mode. By switching between opposite or diagonal port groups, the aperture introduces apparent two-dimensional phase center displacement and supports four information-beam directions: $\varphi=0^\circ$, $45^\circ$, $90^\circ$, and $135^\circ$. An average--differential array factor formulation shows that the average component preserves broad omnidirectional coverage, while the odd-symmetric differential component creates angle-dependent magnitude and phase distortion that determines where the constellation remains recoverable. The recoverable information region is therefore reconfigured without phased-array beamforming, multiple RF chains, or mechanical motion. A 5.05-GHz prototype on Rogers RO4350B is fabricated with an electrical footprint of $0.57 \times 0.47λ_0^2$. Measured 16-QAM results show that low bit error rate is confined to the intended E-plane information-beam sectors, while off-beam angles exhibit large magnitude and phase errors, elevated BER, or unrecoverable constellations despite high received SNR. The measured H-plane cuts maintain low BER over nearly the full angular range, confirming omnidirectional information recovery in the orthogonal plane.

</details>


### [82] [Improving Doppler Resilience of OFDM through Delay-Doppler Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21850)
*Danish Nisar,Saif Khan Mohammed,Muhammad Ubadah,Ronny Hadani,Robert Calderbank*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出基于DD域感知的CP-OFDM方案，通过在数据载波上叠加ZC导频信号，利用DD域信号处理获取时不变的DD域信道滤波器，从而推导出完整的频域输入输出关系，实现ICI均衡，在高移动性场景下显著优于传统CP-OFDM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CP-OFDM在双扩展无线信道中因载间干扰(ICI)导致性能严重下降，而传统时频信号处理方法难以获取完整的频域输入输出关系。

Method: 在CP-OFDM数据载波上叠加Zadoff-Chu(ZC)导频信号；接收端采用DD域信号处理获取有效DD域信道滤波器（在DD域平稳）；从DD域估计推导完整频域输入输出关系；利用该关系估计接收FD导频信号并从接收信号中消除，得到纯数据信号；对纯数据信号进行联合检测以均衡ICI。

Result: 在标准化3GPP TDL-C信道的高移动性场景仿真中，所提DD域感知CP-OFDM方案的频谱效率显著优于传统CP-OFDM。

Conclusion: DD域感知的CP-OFDM通过DD域信号处理有效获取信道信息并消除ICI，在高移动性双扩展信道中能显著提升频谱效率。

Abstract: The performance of traditional CP-OFDM degrades severely in doubly-spread wireless channels due to inter-carrier interference (ICI). In this paper, we propose DD domain sensing based CP-OFDM where we transmit a Zadoff-Chu (ZC) pilot signal overlaid on CP-OFDM data carriers. At the receiver, DD domain signal processing is used to acquire the effective DD domain channel filter which is stationary in the DD domain. From this DD domain estimate, we derive the complete frequency domain (FD) input-output (I/O) relation between CP-OFDM carriers, acquiring which is otherwise difficult with traditional time-frequency signal processing. Using this FD I/O relation, we estimate the received FD pilot signal which is then canceled from the received FD signal, resulting in a data-only signal. Joint detection of all CP-OFDM data carriers from this data-only signal equalizes the effect of ICI. Numerical simulations of the standardized 3GPP TDL-C channel shows that in high mobility scenarios, the proposed DD domain sensing based CP-OFDM achieves significantly better spectral efficiency when compared to that achieved by traditional CP-OFDM.

</details>


### [83] [XMDCA-TL: An Explainable Multi-Domain Channel Attention Transfer Learning Framework for Fault Diagnosis in Industrial Gas Turbines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21991)
*Amir Jahangard Takaloo,Mahdi Aliyari Shoorehdeli,Ehsan Mohammadi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为XMDCA-TL的多任务可解释迁移学习框架，用于工业燃气轮机故障诊断。该方法将振动信号转换为多域RGB图像，通过ConvNeXtV2编码器、多域通道注意力机制、混合掩蔽自监督策略和UNet解码器实现特征提取与重构，并利用迁移学习将实验室知识迁移到实际电厂数据，同时构建了全面的XAI框架进行决策解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业燃气轮机故障诊断面临标注数据稀缺、实际工况噪声干扰大以及模型可解释性不足等挑战，现有方法难以兼顾诊断性能、鲁棒性和物理可解释性。

Method: 首先将振动时域波形转换为包含时域、频域和时频域的多域RGB图像；利用ConvNeXtV2编码器提取特征，并在深层引入MDCA机制建模跨域交互与互补依赖；设计基于混合掩蔽的自监督策略和UNet解码器进行掩蔽区域重构以提升表征质量和抗噪性；采用迁移学习将实验室数据知识迁移至实际电厂（Zahedan电厂42.2 MW MGT-40燃气轮机）数据；构建包含决策区域分析、域重要性评估、域间注意力流和重构不确定性分析的XAI框架。

Result: XMDCA-TL在实现满意故障诊断性能的同时，具备域适应性和噪声鲁棒性，并能提供模型决策过程的物理可解释性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了工业燃气轮机故障诊断中标注数据不足、噪声干扰和模型可解释性差的问题，通过多域特征融合、自监督学习和迁移学习实现了高鲁棒性的诊断，XAI框架增强了模型的透明度和可信度。

Abstract: In this study, a multi-task, interpretable transfer learning framework, XMDCA-TL, is proposed for fault diagnosis in industrial gas turbines. In the proposed method, the vibration time waveform is first converted into a multi-domain RGB representation comprising time, frequency, and time-frequency domains. A ConvNeXtV2-based encoder then processes these images, and the Multi-Domain Channel Attention (MDCA) mechanism is applied to its deep layers to model interactions among different domains and complementary dependencies in the signals. To improve the quality of the learned representations and enhance the model's robustness to noise, a self-supervised strategy based on hybrid masking, along with a UNet-based decoder to reconstruct the masked regions, has been designed. To overcome the limitation of labeled data in industrial environments, transfer learning was employed to transfer knowledge from laboratory data to real-world data from a 42.2 MW MGT-40 gas turbine at the Zahedan power plant. Additionally, a comprehensive Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) framework was developed to analyze decision-making regions, evaluate domain importance, examine the flow of attention between domains, and assess reconstruction uncertainty. The results showed that XMDCA-TL, while achieving satisfactory fault diagnosis performance, possesses domain adaptability and robustness to noise and provides a physical interpretation of the model's decision-making process.

</details>


### [84] [Full-Domain Coupler: A Wireless Native Neural Backbone for Channel Representation and Deduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22038)
*Zirui Chen,Ziqing Xing,Zhaoyang Zhang,Hongning Ruan,Yuzhi Yang,Zhaohui Yang,Chongwen Huang,Merouane Debbah*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出Coupler，一种面向无线信道状态信息（CSI）表征学习的原生AI神经主干，通过逐层解耦多域学习与维度交错耦合机制，实现高参数效率和细粒度多域特征融合，在信道推测任务上验证了显著的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线AI技术缺乏专用的数据表征方案，主要依赖拼接通用网络，导致结构冗余和信息流瓶颈。为填补这一空白，需要为无线系统核心数据CSI设计原生AI表征学习架构。

Method: Coupler基于信道张量的物理先验，将表征学习逐层分解到各独立域，再通过维度交错级联耦合域特定特征，形成全域交错学习架构。使用复数域MLP作为空间/频域学习器，采用卷积、注意力或门控三种机制捕获时间依赖。

Result: 在信道估计、插值、预测和反馈等信道推测任务上，基于Coupler的系列方案显著优于基线，参数效率高、模型轻量化，在实测数据上同样表现稳健。

Conclusion: Coupler展示了作为无线基础模型基本架构的潜力，具备紧凑性、通用性和灵活性，为无线原生AI表征学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Data representation is a fundamental issue in deep learning. However, as wireless data scales and deeply couples across many physical domains such as time, space, and frequency, existing wireless artificial intelligence (AI) technologies lack dedicated representation solutions. Instead, they mainly rely on stitching general-purpose networks, a tool-driven paradigm that inevitably results in structural redundancy and bottlenecks in information flow. To fill this gap, this paper proposes Coupler, a wireless native-AI neural backbone designed for representation learning of channel state information (CSI)--the pivotal data in wireless systems. Leveraging the revealed physical insights of channel tensors, Coupler decomposes representation learning into individual domains on a layer-by-layer basis, and then couples the learned domain-specific features through a dimension-staggered cascade. This full-domain interleaved learning architecture enables superior parameter efficiency and fine-grained multi-domain feature fusion. Based on this backbone, we use the complex-domain multilayer perceptrons (CMLPs) as spatial and frequency domain learners, while employing three optional mechanisms--convolution, attention, or gating--to capture temporal dependencies. This results in a series of efficient channel learning schemes with diverse functionalities and extreme lightweights, showcasing the compactness, versatility and flexibility of Coupler. We evaluate these schemes on channel deduction, a general representation task encompassing channel estimation, interpolation, prediction, and feedback. Extensive experimental evaluations validate their significant performance gains and robust applicability even for real-world measured data, demonstrating the potential of Coupler as a promising basic architecture in the design of wireless foundation models.

</details>


### [85] [Anticipating the Optimism Gap: Predicting Distribution-Shift Degradation of RF-Impairment Detectors from In-Distribution Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22054)
*Chakshu Baweja*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究GNSS射频干扰检测器在分布外数据上的性能乐观偏差（optimism gap），发现该偏差可通过仅使用分布内数据的评分统计量进行预测（R²≈0.47），并在合成测试床和三个实地语料库上验证了该机制的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS射频干扰检测器（抗干扰、抗欺骗、抗多径）通常在 tuned 的分布上报告单一AUC指标，但实际条件变化后性能会下降，且由于标注现场数据稀缺，下降幅度难以预先知晓。本文旨在探索这种乐观偏差是否可以在未见任何分布外数据之前被预测。

Method: 基于开放的参数化合成测试床，对13种检测器（5种物理基线、全特征逻辑回归与MLP、单特征学习控制）在4类干扰上进行评估。定义乐观差距（optimism gap）为分布内AUC与移位后AUC之差。使用仅基于分布内评分统计量的岭回归模型预测未见过的检测器或未见过的干扰类别的乐观差距。在合成数据上验证后，在三个开放现场语料库（Jammertest 2024、SatGrid等）上运行预注册协议。

Result: 乐观差距随移位的加深单调增长（平均Spearman相关性0.50）；驱动因素主要是检测器使用的可观测特征数量而非是否为学习型；岭回归模型对未见检测器预测R²=0.47，对未见干扰类别预测R²=0.46（均经2000次置换检验p<0.001）；在Jammertest 2024上交叉检测器预测R²=0.11（p=0.009）；SatGrid上分布内AUC高估高严重性AUC达0.22，出现符号反转，分布内AUC与实现差距完美秩相关（Spearman rho=1.0）。

Conclusion: 该乐观偏差预测机制在真实数据中仍然有效（尽管幅度小于仿真），表明仅依赖分布内统计量即可预测检测器在分布外数据上的性能下降程度。研究发布了测试床、软件接收前端、数据适配器和协议。

Abstract: Detectors for GNSS radio-frequency impairments (jamming, spoofing, multipath) are usually reported with a single AUC measured on the distribution they were tuned on. That number falls once conditions move, and the size of the drop is rarely known in advance because labelled field data is scarce. We ask whether this optimism can be predicted before any out-of-distribution data is seen. On an open, parameter-grounded synthetic testbed with a tunable severity shift, we evaluate thirteen detectors (five physics baselines, full-feature logistic regression and multilayer perceptrons, and single-feature learned controls) across four impairment classes. The optimism gap, the difference between in-distribution and shifted AUC, grows monotonically as the shift deepens (mean Spearman correlation 0.50). It is driven by how many observables a detector uses rather than by whether it is learned, and it varies systematically by class. Centrally, a ridge model built only from in-distribution score statistics predicts the gap for a detector it has never seen (R^2 = 0.47) and for an impairment class it has never seen (R^2 = 0.46); both are significant against a 2000-fold permutation null (p < 0.001) and survive removing the feature that is, by construction, part of the target. The headline findings are synthetic. We then run the pre-registered protocol on three open field corpora: on Jammertest 2024 the cross-detector prediction holds (R^2 = 0.11, p = 0.009), and on SatGrid, whose spoofer power sweep gives a calibrated severity axis, in-distribution AUC overstates higher-severity AUC by up to 0.22 and to the point of sign inversion, with in-distribution AUC and realised gap perfectly rank-correlated (Spearman rho = 1.0). The mechanism survives contact with real data, at smaller magnitude than in simulation. We release the testbed, a software-receiver front end, the ingest adapters and the protocol.

</details>


### [86] [A Novel Grant Prediction Method for 5G NR Terminals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22125)
*Chenhao Wu,Xiaojiang Xu,Yuxuan Li,Yuanhao Xu,Wenhui Xiong,Xiaoyu Fu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对5G NR用户设备因持续PDCCH监听导致高功耗的问题，本文提出IOHMM-BO模型（高阶输入-输出隐马尔可夫模型结合贝叶斯优化），通过复合状态捕获长程依赖，联合优化模型阶数和监听窗口。基于真实5G NR轨迹实验，实现了45.3%准确率、5.0%假阴性率和43%节能，在可靠性与能效间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G NR用户设备因持续PDCCH监听导致功耗过高，现有预测性动态电源管理（DPM）方法面临调度状态不可观测和数据授权模式突发性的挑战，难以实现准确预测。

Method: 提出IOHMM-BO模型：采用高阶输入-输出隐马尔可夫模型（IOHMM）通过复合状态捕获长程依赖关系；利用贝叶斯优化（BO）联合优化模型阶数和监听窗口参数。

Result: 在真实5G NR轨迹上，达到45.3%预测准确率、5.0%假阴性率、43%能量节省，且计算开销较低。

Conclusion: IOHMM-BO在可靠性与能效之间实现了良好的平衡性折中，为5G NR设备的动态电源管理提供了一种有效的预测方案。

Abstract: 5G NR user equipment suffers from high power consumption due to continuous PDCCH monitoring. Predictive dynamic power management (DPM) can save energy by forecasting data grants, but accurate prediction is challenging due to unobservable scheduling states and bursty grant patterns. This paper proposes IOHMM-BO, a high-order input-output hidden Markov model with Bayesian optimization. Based on real 5G NR traces, we capture long-range dependencies via a compound state and jointly optimize model order and listening window using Bayesian optimization. Experiments on real traces show that IOHMM-BO achieves 45.3% accuracy, 5.0% false negative rate, and 43% energy saving with low computational overhead. The method provides a balanced trade-off between reliability and energy efficiency.

</details>


### [87] [RemoteRF: An Open-Source Platform to Democratize Access to Software-Defined Radios in Wireless Research and Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22161)
*Ethan Y. Ge,Ian P. Roberts*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: RemoteRF是一个开源平台，通过单一服务器集中管理分布式软件无线电（SDR）设备，允许用户通过网络远程访问和进行无线通信实验，已在UCLA累计近4000小时使用时长，服务200多名学生和研究人员。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件无线电（SDR）设备成本高、复杂度大，使得许多大学和研究人员难以获取和使用，需要一种更经济、便捷的远程访问方案来降低使用门槛。

Method: 提出RemoteRF平台，采用中心服务器统一管理分布式SDR节点，用户通过互联网远程访问这些设备进行无线实验；平台软件开源并配有详细文档，支持快速部署。

Result: 在UCLA经过一年多的教学和研究应用，累计近4000小时使用时长，服务超过200名学生和研究人员。

Conclusion: RemoteRF有效降低了SDR的使用门槛，实现了全球范围内的SDR资源共享，在无线通信研究和教育中具有重要推广价值。

Abstract: Software-defined radios (SDRs) are powerful tools for research and education in wireless communications, but their cost and complexity put them out of reach for many universities and researchers worldwide. To address this, we introduce RemoteRF, a platform for creating large-scale testbeds of distributed SDRs that are centrally managed by a single server. These SDRs can be remotely accessed by users over the internet, allowing them to conduct wireless experiments at any time from virtually anywhere, as long as they have a network connection. When used in research, RemoteRF can be used to develop and experimentally evaluate new communication techniques or to collect real-world data to train and test machine learning models. When used in education, RemoteRF can allow students in virtually any sized class to share a handful of SDRs to complete active learning lab exercises that parallel course lectures. In an effort to democratize access to SDRs across the globe, the software powering RemoteRF has been made open-source and is extensively documented, allowing anyone to deploy their own instance today in a matter of minutes. Over the past year or so, RemoteRF has been used in both teaching and research at UCLA, where it has logged nearly 4,000 hours of use by more than 200 students and researchers to date.

</details>


### [88] [Outage Analysis and Fairness Design for Spatially Correlated FAS-Enabled RSMA Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22287)
*Jinyuan Liu,Yong Liang Guan,Tuo Wu,Hong Niu,Kai-Kit Wong,Bruno Clerckx*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了在6G多用户MISO下行链路中结合速率分割多址接入（RSMA）与流体天线系统（FAS）的方案，推导了闭式中断概率表达式，并提出了以最小化最差用户中断概率为目标的公平性优化算法。数值结果表明，相比固定位置天线基线，FAS-RSMA可降低高达92%的中断概率，并在公平性设计下为最差用户带来1 dB的SNR增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中缺乏结合RSMA与FAS的闭式中断概率表达式以及可处理的多用户公平性优化方法，而这对于6G系统实现更高可靠性、更密集连接和更优干扰控制至关重要。

Method: 采用块相关性建立FAS空间相关模型，结合迫零和最大比传输线性预编码，利用单因子构造和广义Gauss-Laguerre求积推导闭式中断概率表达式；基于此提出以最小化最差用户中断概率为目标的低复杂度算法，每次迭代通过线性规划可行性检查获得闭式解。

Result: 在不同端口数、信道条件和目标速率下验证了理论分析，FAS-RSMA相比固定位置天线基线降低中断概率高达92%，公平性设计在均衡用户可靠性的同时为最差用户带来1 dB SNR增益。

Conclusion: RSMA与FAS的联合方案能够有效提升多用户MISO网络的干扰管理和空间选择性，且所提公平性优化算法可在低复杂度下实现用户间可靠性均衡。

Abstract: Sixth-generation (6G) systems target higher reliability, denser connectivity, and tighter interference control. {Within this context, rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA) is envisioned as a promising candidate to enhance interference management in future wireless networks by flexibly splitting messages into a common and a private part, while fluid antenna systems (FAS) offer the potential to improve spatial selectivity through dynamic port reconfiguration.} Combining RSMA and FAS therefore enables efficient interference control and adaptive antenna utilization in multiuser multi-input single-output (MISO) networks. However, deriving closed-form outage probability (OP) expressions and tractable user fairness optimization in this scenario remains scarce in the literature. This paper studies a multiuser MISO downlink that jointly leverages RSMA and FAS. We develop a spatial correlation model for FAS using block correlation and incorporate linear precoding with zero-forcing and maximum-ratio transmission. Within this model, we derive closed-form OP expressions using a one-factor construction and generalized Gauss-Laguerre quadrature. Building on these expressions, we formulate a fairness objective that minimizes the worst-user OP and propose a low-complexity algorithm with a linear-program feasibility check to obtain the closed-form solution per iteration. Numerical results across different port counts, channel conditions, and target rates validate the analytical analysis, show that FAS-RSMA reduces OP by up to 92% relative to the fixed-position antenna (FPA) baseline, and demonstrate that fairness-oriented design equalizes user reliability while delivering a 1 dB SNR gain for the worst user at a fixed outage level.

</details>


### [89] [Towards Whole Hand and Wrist Kinematic Tracking with a Wearable A-Mode Ultrasound Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22333)
*Giusy Spacone,Luca Benini,Andrea Cossettini*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出基于可穿戴A-mode超声平台的WULPUS，用于手部和腕部23自由度运动跟踪。通过紧凑型CNN（11285参数）结合增量训练策略，将平均绝对误差降低17%以上；首次实现端到端运动跟踪完全在设备端运行（nRF52832微控制器），每次推理仅0.73 mJ、延迟29.1 ms，总功耗33 mW，支持36小时连续使用，无线带宽降低88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有A-mode超声手势追踪工作主要局限于静态手势分类或少数自由度的回归，且依赖非可穿戴系统和外部计算设备，同时传感器重新放置后的鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出WULPUS可穿戴A-mode US平台；设计紧凑型多输出CNN（11285参数）；采用增量训练策略改善跨会话泛化；模型部署于nRF52832微控制器实现完全在设备端推理。

Result: 增量训练相比非增量方法MAE降低17%以上；在设备端推理能耗0.73 mJ/次，延迟29.1 ms；系统总功耗33 mW，可连续使用36小时；无线带宽降低88%。

Conclusion: 首次证明了利用可穿戴A-mode超声实现23自由度手部和腕部运动跟踪完全在设备端运行的可行性，为长期、低功耗、高鲁棒性的手势追踪提供了可行方案。

Abstract: A-mode ultrasound (US) has emerged as a promising modality for hand and wrist motion tracking. Prior works have mainly addressed static gesture classification or regression of a few degrees of freedom (DoFs), typically relying on non-wearable systems and external computing devices, and highlight the need for strategies to ensure robustness to sensor repositioning. In this work, we propose a framework for robust whole-hand and wrist kinematic tracking via wearable A-mode US using the WULPUS platform, tackling the regression of 23 DoFs directly on the probe. First, we introduce a compact (11285 parameters) multi-output convolutional neural network combined with an incremental training strategy, which improves inter-session generalization and reduces mean absolute error by more than 17% compared to a non-incremental approach. Second, we demonstrate, for the first time, the feasibility of end-to-end hand and wrist kinematic tracking entirely on-device. We deploy the model on the WULPUS nRF52832 microcontroller, achieving 0.73 mJ per inference, 29.1 ms latency, and showing the feasibility of full operation (data acquisition, online inference, and BLE streaming of results) within 33 mW, enabling up to 36 hours of continuous use and an 88% reduction in wireless bandwidth compared to raw data transmission.

</details>


### [90] [PAPR Reduction for AFDM by Affine-Domain Circular Shift without Side Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22464)
*The Khai Nguyen,Ebrahim Bedeer*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种无需边信息即可降低仿射频分复用（AFDM）信号峰均比（PAPR）的新方法，通过仿射域循环移位选择最低PAPR候选，并设计最大似然接收机盲检测移位量，实现2.5-4dB的PAPR降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AFDM信号具有高PAPR问题，现有降低PAPR的方法通常需要传输边信息，增加了系统开销和复杂度，因此需要一种无需边信息的PAPR降低方案。

Method: 在仿射域对原始发射信号进行循环移位，生成多个候选信号并选择PAPR最低者；接收端利用AFDM导频和保护带的位置信息，设计基于最大似然（MLB）的接收机，无需边信息即可检测发射端施加的移位量。

Result: 与原始AFDM及现有方案相比，该方法可实现2.5至4dB的PAPR降低；根据功率放大器质量的不同，可转化为显著更低的误码率。

Conclusion: 所提出的无需边信息的循环移位PAPR降低方法在AFDM系统中有效可行，能显著降低PAPR并改善系统误码率性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel method to reduce the peak-to-average-power-ratio (PAPR) of affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM) signals without side information (SI). The method is based on circularly shifting the original transmit signal in the affine domain, and selecting the shifted candidate with the lowest PAPR. Next, a maximum-likelihood-based (MLB) receiver is derived, which exploits the position of the AFDM pilot and guard band to detect the shift applied at the transmitter without SI. Simulation results show that the proposed method can achieve 2.5 to 4 dB in PAPR reduction as compared to original AFDM and existing schemes, which can be translated into significantly lower error rate, depending on the quality of power amplifiers.

</details>


### [91] [Generative Site-Specific Beamforming for UPAs via Decoupled Channel Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22522)
*Yao Tang,Zhaolin Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种交叉融合生成式特定站点波束赋形（GenSSBF）框架，通过解耦的方位/仰角信道感知策略将UPA系统的波束对准开销从乘法复杂度降至线性，并利用双向交叉注意力编码器融合解耦观测的潜在依赖，结合条件归一化流生成器产生高保真波束候选集，在DeepMIMO场景中以93.8%的扫描开销降低实现了相比全1024波束DFT搜索最高83.6%的归一化波束赋形增益提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统UPA系统需要二维DFT码本穷举扫描，波束对准开销呈乘法复杂度增长（方位×仰角），且解耦感知虽能降低开销但会丢失方位-仰角耦合信息，导致波束生成高度模糊。

Method: 1) 解耦信道感知策略：独立探测UPA的方位和仰角域，将扫描开销从乘法复杂度降为线性；2) 双向交叉注意力编码器：提取并融合方位与仰角感知分支间的潜在依赖关系；3) 条件归一化流生成器：基于融合特征生成紧凑的高保真波束候选集；4) 轻量级导频验证：对候选集进行少量导频测量确定最终波束；5) 任务导向训练目标：鼓励生成候选集至少包含一个高增益波束。

Result: 在DeepMIMO的I2_28、O1B_28和Boston5G_28场景中，相比全1024波束二维DFT搜索，归一化波束赋形增益分别提升83.6%、74.6%和38.1%，扫描开销降低93.8%。

Conclusion: GenSSBF框架在极低扫描开销下显著优于确定性波束预测和传统DFT码本搜索，验证了解耦感知+交叉注意力融合+生成式波束候选集方案在UPA系统中的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: A cross-fused generative site-specific beamforming (GenSSBF) framework is proposed for low-overhead beam alignment in uniform planar array (UPA) systems. A decoupled channel sensing strategy is developed, where the azimuth and elevation domains of the UPA are probed independently, and the online sweeping overhead is reduced from multiplicative to linear complexity compared to exhaustive two-dimensional codebook sweeping. However, the resulting reference signal received power (RSRP) observations only contain marginal angular power information. The explicit azimuth-elevation coupling of the UPA channel is therefore lost. Beam generation from these separate observations becomes highly ambiguous. To address this issue, a bidirectional cross-attention encoder is designed to extract and fuse the latent dependency between the azimuth and elevation sensing branches. Conditioned on the fused feature, a conditional normalizing flow generator is proposed to generate a compact set of high-fidelity beam candidates. These candidates are further verified through lightweight pilot measurements for final beam selection. A task-oriented training objective is also introduced to encourage the generated candidate set to contain at least one high-gain beam, rather than fitting the full conditional beam distribution. Simulation results based on DeepMIMO scenarios show that the proposed framework consistently outperforms deterministic beam prediction and conventional discrete Fourier transform (DFT) codebook search. Compared with the full 1024-beam two-dimensional DFT search, normalized beamforming gain improvements of 83.6%, 74.6%, and 38.1% are achieved in the I2_28, O1B_28, and Boston5G_28 scenarios, respectively, while the sweeping overhead is reduced by 93.8%.

</details>


### [92] [Low-Complexity Direct Geolocation of Terrestrial GNSS Jammers from Low Earth Orbit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22751)
*Giacomo Pojani,Javier Tegedor,Joaquim Fortuny-Guasch*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为准直接地理定位（QDG）的低复杂度技术，用于对射频发射器进行直接位置域定位，并在Jammertest 2025中通过OPS-SAT PRETTY卫星成功验证了其对地面干扰源的定位能力。QDG大幅降低了直接地理定位（DG/DPD）的复杂度，可运行于低SWaP卫星平台，作为数据采集器压缩I/Q样本或作为边缘计算节点在轨计算干扰源位置，但灵敏度和精度低于DG/DPD，且仅适用于包括最常见GNSS干扰源在内的特定信号类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直接地理定位（DG/DPD）技术复杂度高，难以在受限于低尺寸、重量和功率（SWaP）的低地球轨道（LEO）卫星上部署。为了实现卫星作为多星座系统中的机会频谱传感器，对全球导航卫星系统（GNSS）频段的射频干扰（RFI）进行监测，需要一种低复杂度的替代方案。

Method: 提出准直接地理定位（QDG）技术，在位置域直接进行发射器定位，通过压缩大量I/Q采样数据为最小信号信息以降低复杂度。支持两种模式：作为数据采集器通过低容量下行链路将压缩数据传输至地面后处理；作为边缘计算机在星载低功耗计算机（OBC）上直接计算干扰源位置。

Result: 在Jammertest 2025中，利用改造后的LEO卫星OPS-SAT PRETTY成功实现了对地面干扰源的地理定位，实验验证了QDG的有效性。代价是灵敏度和精度低于DG/DPD，且可定位的信号源类型有限，但其中包含了最常见的GNSS干扰源。

Conclusion: QDG为低SWaP卫星提供了参与RFI监测的可行方案，虽性能有所折衷，但足以应对最常见的GNSS干扰场景，为多星座RFI监测系统提供了机会频谱传感器的新途径。

Abstract: This paper introduces a low-complexity technique named quasi-direct geolocation (QDG) to perform passive radio-frequency (RF) geolocation of emitters directly in the position domain, akin to direct geolocation (DG) and direct position determination (DPD). The proposed technique drastically reduces the complexity of DG/DPD and is experimentally demonstrated in geolocating a terrestrial jammer at Jammertest 2025 from a repurposed satellite in low Earth orbit (LEO): OPS-SAT PRETTY. The goal of QDG is to enable satellites to contribute to a multi-constellation system for RF interference (RFI) monitoring as opportunistic spectrum sensors in global navigation satellite systems (GNSS) bands, even if these are constrained by low size, weight, and power (SWaP). They can serve as data collectors and/or edge computers. In the former case, QDG can be used to compress large volumes of I/Q samples into minimal signal information, which can be relayed to ground for post-processing via low-capacity downlinks. In the latter case, QDG can be used to compute the geolocation of RFI sources in orbit on low-power on-board computers (OBC). The drawback of these capabilities is lower sensitivity and accuracy than DG/DPD plus limitations on the types of signal sources that can be geolocated, which, nonetheless, include the most common GNSS jammers.

</details>


### [93] [Radio Resource Allocation for Beam Hopping Scheduling in LEO Satellite Communications: A Spatio-Temporal Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22900)
*Hao Yuan,Lanyining Li,Jianghua Long,Xing Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对低轨卫星网络中动态流量需求和干扰约束下的无线资源分配问题，提出了一种基于禁忌搜索的时空波束跳变资源分配策略，通过自适应禁忌控制、贪婪初始化和模拟退火接受准则，在满足干扰约束下最大化用户需求满意度。仿真表明，相比贪婪策略，系统吞吐量提升17.2%，用户满意度提升11.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨卫星网络面临动态流量需求和严格干扰约束下的无线资源分配挑战，现有方法难以同时满足时域和空域的高效调度。

Method: 提出基于禁忌搜索的时空波束跳变资源分配框架，包含三个关键组件：自适应禁忌步长控制、基于干扰感知波束选择的贪婪初始化、以及模拟退火接受准则，将BH调度问题建模为干扰约束下的用户需求满意度最大化问题。

Result: 相比贪婪波束跳变策略，系统吞吐量提升17.2%，用户满意度提升11.7%。

Conclusion: 所提方法为干扰受限的低轨卫星网络提供了可扩展且鲁棒的动态资源分配解决方案。

Abstract: Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite networks face critical challenges in radio resource allocation due to dynamic traffic demands and stringent interference constraints. Beam-hopping (BH) technology offers a promising solution by enabling dynamic beam resource allocation across spatial and temporal domains. In this paper, we propose a Tabu Search-based spatio-temporal BH resource allocation strategy for LEO satellite communication systems. Specifically, the BH scheduling problem is formulated to maximize user demand satisfaction under interference constraints. To solve this problem efficiently, the proposed Tabu Search framework integrates adaptive tabu tenure control, greedy-based initialization with interference-aware beam selection, and Simulated Annealing acceptance criteria. Extensive simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method consistently improves system throughput by 17.2\% and user satisfaction by 11.7\% compared with greedy-based BH strategies. These results indicate that the proposed approach provides a scalable and robust solution for dynamic resource allocation in interference-limited LEO satellite networks.

</details>


### [94] [Integrating Sensing into Covert Communications: Opportunities and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23073)
*Jun Wu,Xiaoqi Zhang,Haoyuan Pan,Gaosheng Zhao,Dong In Kim,Tse-Tin Chan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文探讨了感知赋能的隐蔽通信，利用敌方和环境信息引导传输与干扰控制，通过感知辅助波束成形在时变信道中提升空间资源利用率和隐蔽传输可靠性，并讨论了相关挑战与开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于盲干扰或被动不确定性的隐蔽通信设计在动态传播环境中效果不佳，需要更智能的感知机制来提升隐蔽通信的适应性和有效性。

Method: 提出感知赋能的隐蔽通信框架，利用环境感知获取敌方和信道信息，指导传输策略与干扰控制；讨论多种智能感知范式以有限主动探测提取任务相关信令；并通过低空无线网络案例验证感知辅助波束成形的效果。

Result: 感知辅助波束成形在时变信道中能够改善空间资源利用率并提升隐蔽数据传输的可靠性。

Conclusion: 感知将隐蔽系统从被动隐藏转变为状态感知决策，但仍面临暴露风险和资源消耗等新挑战；需要进一步研究以支撑更自适应的隐蔽无线系统。

Abstract: Covert communications aim to hide the existence of wireless transmissions from unauthorized adversaries. However, conventional designs based on blind interference or passive uncertainty can be ineffective in dynamic propagation environments. This article investigates sensing-empowered covert communications, where adversary and environmental information are used to guide transmission and jamming control. We show how sensing changes covert system design from passive concealment to state-aware decision-making, while also introducing new challenges related to exposure and resource consumption. We further discuss several intelligent sensing paradigms that extract task-relevant information with limited active probing. A case study in low-altitude wireless networks illustrates that sensing-assisted beamforming can improve spatial resource utilization and the reliability of covert data delivery in time-varying channels. Finally, several open issues are discussed to support more adaptive covert wireless systems.

</details>


### [95] [LOLLA: Deep Reinforcement Learning for Closed-Loop Link Adaptation Towards a GPU-Accelerated AI-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23110)
*Rui Wang,Linchao Zhang,Qiang Liu,Kun Yang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出LOLLA框架，利用深度强化学习替代传统OLLA算法，通过连续SINR偏移和PPO策略实现5G NR链路自适应，在高速移动场景下相比OLLA获得15%-92%吞吐量提升，并严格支配OLLA的Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OLLA依赖一阶单比特反馈，在高移动性和快变信道条件下性能显著下降，亟需更高效的链路自适应方案。

Method: 采用深度强化学习（PPO）学习连续SINR偏移，利用丰富的PHY/MAC遥测信息，在拉格朗日BLER约束下自动实现可调可靠性目标，并将其部署在GPU加速的5G NR协议栈上。

Result: 在3GPP TDL信道模型下，Doppler频率高达400 Hz时，相比OLLA获得15%-92%吞吐量提升；严格支配OLLA的Pareto前沿；泛化到未见信道模型；支持8个并发UE。

Conclusion: LOLLA作为一种AI原生闭环控制方案，在保持3GPP兼容性的同时，显著优于传统OLLA，且上行链路可实现仿真到部署的一致性。

Abstract: Outer-loop link adaptation (OLLA) is widely deployed in 5G NR to track channel variations, yet its reliance on first-order, single-bit feedback degrades performance significantly under high-mobility and fast-varying channels. This paper presents LOLLA (Learned Outer-Loop Link Adaptation), a deep reinforcement learning framework that replaces the conventional OLLA staircase with a learned, continuous SINR offset conditioned on rich PHY/MAC telemetry inaccessible to OLLA. The offset modulates the SINR-to-MCS lookup table, preserving 3GPP-compliant MCS selection and provably subsuming the conventional OLLA update rule. A Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) policy trained under a Lagrangian block error rate (BLER) constraint automatically enforces tunable reliability targets from 1% to 15% without manual penalty calibration. The framework is realized as the first closed-loop AI-native control dApp on a GPU-accelerated 5G NR stack, achieving end-to-end control latencies under 500 microseconds. Evaluations under 3GPP TDL channel models demonstrate 15% to 92% throughput gains over OLLA across Doppler frequencies up to 400 Hz, while attaining a Pareto frontier that strictly dominates OLLA across all evaluated reliability targets. The learned policy generalizes to unseen channel models and scales to eight concurrent UEs under shared-resource scheduling. In the uplink formulation, the gNB directly observes decoding outcomes, enabling simulation-to-deployment parity.

</details>


### [96] [Fault Inception Detection in Real-World Disturbance Data for Power System Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23111)
*Julian Oelhaf,Mehran Pashaei,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro,Georg Kordowich,Christian Bergler,Andreas Maier,Johann Jaeger,Siming Bayer*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的真实输电网络故障起始检测框架，结合保护域指标、鲁棒归一化、低延迟瞬态路径及持久性感知融合逻辑，在12053个RTE数据库记录上取得了96.6%召回率、79.2%精确率和4.2ms中位定时误差，可用于保护导向的扰动筛查和事件标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实输电网络中的扰动记录缺乏精确的事件起始时间标注，且现场波形中包含开关操作、变压器励磁、谐振、饱和等非理想效应，这些效应可能掩盖或模仿真实故障特征，使得可靠的故障起始检测变得困难，限制了这些数据在保护与自动扰动分析中的价值。

Method: 提出了一种无需训练的框架，包含：(1)保护域指标提取；(2)基于中位数/MAD的鲁棒归一化；(3)低延迟瞬态路径；(4)持久性感知融合与否决逻辑，用于区分故障一致性扰动与非故障暂态。

Result: 在RTE数据库12053个输电级记录上测试，并在300个事件的经人工审查子集上评估：召回率96.6%，精确率79.2%，匹配检测的中位定时误差为4.2ms。

Conclusion: 该方法能够支持保护导向的扰动筛查、继电器和事后事件分析，并为下游数据驱动的监测任务创建时间戳标注。

Abstract: Large collections of real-world disturbance recordings are increasingly available in transmission networks, but their value for power system protection and automated disturbance analysis is limited by the absence of precise event-onset annotations. In practice, field-recorded voltage and current waveforms contain switching operations, transformer energization, resonance, saturation, and other non-ideal effects that can obscure or mimic genuine fault signatures, making reliable fault inception detection difficult. This paper presents an training-free framework for fault inception detection in real-world transmission disturbance data. The method combines protection-domain indicators, robust median/MAD-based normalization, a low-latency transient path, and persistence-aware fusion and veto logic to distinguish fault-consistent disturbances from non-fault transients. We apply the framework to 12053 transmission-level recordings from the publicly available RTE database and further assess detector performance on a manually reviewed subset of 300 events. On the reviewed subset, the detector achieves 96.6% recall, 79.2% precision, and a median timing error of 4.2ms for matched detections. These results indicate that the proposed approach can support protection-oriented disturbance screening, relay and post-event analysis, and the creation of timestamp annotations for downstream data-driven monitoring tasks.

</details>


### [97] [AI-Empowered UAV-Assisted Backscatter Localization and ISAC for Zero-Energy IoT: A Comprehensive Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23125)
*Ruhul Amin Khalil*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI赋能的无人机辅助反向散射定位与通感一体化技术在零能耗物联网中的应用，涵盖网络架构、无人机角色、反向散射模式、射频源、定位与感知功能、AI技术及性能指标，并讨论了开放挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零能耗物联网依赖能量采集而非电池供电，但传统反向散射通信存在反射弱、双路径损耗大、覆盖有限、直射链路干扰及对外部射频源依赖等缺陷。无人机作为移动载体可缓解这些局限，而通感一体化能进一步共享无线资源实现数据传输、定位与感知。本文旨在系统综述该交叉领域的技术现状与挑战。

Method: 采用PRISMA指导的结构化综述方法，构建统一分类体系（网络架构、无人机角色、反向散射模式、射频源、定位与感知功能、AI技术、性能指标），通过对比表格、定量趋势分析、覆盖评估及教程式数值示例进行技术讨论。

Result: 系统梳理了无人机辅助反向散射通信、无源定位、通感一体化无人机-反向散射系统及AI驱动优化等关键技术，提供了技术比较与趋势分析，并识别了当前研究空白。

Conclusion: 指出了开放挑战与未来方向，包括真实信道建模、能量中性运行、基准测试与可复现性、可扩展可信AI、安全隐私、硬件验证，以及与RIS、MEC、数字孪生、6G技术的融合。

Abstract: Zero-energy Internet of Things (IoT) enables passive or near-passive devices to operate on harvested energy rather than batteries. Backscatter communication (BackCom) supports this vision by enabling tags to transmit data via reflection and modulation of incident RF signals, but it suffers from weak reflections, double-path loss, limited coverage, direct-link interference, and dependence on external RF sources. Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) can mitigate these limitations by acting as mobile carrier emitters, data collectors, relays, aerial receivers, mobile anchors, sensing platforms, and edge-intelligence nodes. Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) further enables the sharing of wireless resources for data transmission, localization, target sensing, and environmental awareness. This article surveys RF-based AI-empowered UAV-assisted backscatter localization and ISAC for zero-energy IoT. It reviews enabling technologies, presents a structured PRISMA-informed methodology, and develops a unified taxonomy covering network architectures, UAV roles, backscatter modes, RF sources, localization and sensing functions, AI techniques, and performance metrics. It also discusses UAV-assisted BackCom, passive localization, ISAC-enabled UAV-backscatter systems, and AI-driven optimization through comparative tables, quantitative trend analysis, coverage evaluation, and tutorial-style numerical illustrations. Finally, it identifies open challenges and future directions in realistic channel modeling, energy-neutral operation, benchmarking, reproducibility, scalable and trustworthy AI, security, privacy, hardware validation, and integration with RIS, MEC, digital twins, and 6G technologies.

</details>


### [98] [When Distortion Helps: Secure GNN Precoding with Nonlinear Power Amplifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23141)
*Reza Ghasemi Alavicheh,Thomas Feys,Md Arifur Rahman,François Rottenberg*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图神经网络（GNN）的预编码框架，利用功率放大器（PA）非线性失真作为安全资产，在多用户MISO窃听信道中最大化保密和速率，无需窃听者信道状态信息和人工噪声功率分配。在PA严重饱和条件下（IBO=-1dB, SNR=22dB），相比MRT、ZF及AN辅助方案，保密和速率提升8.67%-39.89%，且方差降低58.13%-75.31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理层安全技术假设理想线性发射机，但实际功率放大器会引入非线性失真，通常被视为有害因素。本文旨在证明这种失真可以被重新利用为安全资产，特别是在功率高效的PA饱和区域，通过将其定向引导至窃听者来增强安全性。

Method: 提出GNN-based预编码框架，利用Bussgang分解和高阶多项式PA模型构建可解析的保密和速率作为训练目标，从合法用户信道数据直接学习预编码策略，无需窃听者CSI或专门的人工噪声功率分配。

Result: 在22 dB SNR、PA严重饱和（IBO=-1 dB）条件下，GNN方案相比MRT、ZF、AN-MRT、AN-ZF分别提升39.89%、35.26%、17.99%、8.67%的保密和速率，且标准偏差降低58.13%-75.31%。

Conclusion: PA非线性失真可以在PLS中作为可利用的安全资源，GNN框架能有效学习复杂非线性场景下的预编码策略，优于传统线性及AN辅助方案。

Abstract: Physical layer security (PLS) provides information-theoretic protection against eavesdropping. While existing techniques assume ideal linear transmitters, power amplifiers (PAs) in practice introduce nonlinear distortion, typically considered detrimental to signal quality. This paper demonstrates that such distortion can instead be exploited as a security asset by redirecting it toward eavesdroppers, particularly in the power-efficient PA saturation regime. To this end, we propose a graph neural network (GNN)-based precoding framework for multi-user multiple-input single-output (MISO) wiretap channels that maximizes the sum secrecy rate by exploiting PA nonlinearity. Since the resulting optimization is highly non-convex, classical methods are intractable. The GNN instead learns precoding strategies directly from legitimate users' channel data, requiring neither eavesdropper channel state information (CSI) nor dedicated artificial noise (AN) power allocation. For this, the Bussgang decomposition and a high-order polynomial PA model provide an analytical secrecy rate as the training objective. At 22 dB signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) under severe PA saturation with input back-off (IBO) $= -1$ dB, the proposed GNN achieves 39.89% and 35.26% higher sum secrecy rate over maximum ratio transmission (MRT) and zero-forcing (ZF), respectively, 17.99% over AN-aided MRT and 8.67% over AN-aided ZF, with 58.13-75.31% lower standard deviation across all baselines.

</details>


### [99] [Phase Uniformity Detector for GRSMReceivers in mmWave and Sub-THz Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23182)
*Oshin Daoud,Haifa Fares,Yahia Medjahdi,Laurent Clavier,Amor Nafkha*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向广义接收空间调制（GRSM）系统的相位域统计检测器——相位均匀性检测器（PUD），通过直接射频采样和方向统计建模，推导出广义似然比检验并简化为瑞利均匀性检验，具有闭式、与噪声方差无关的阈值，相比传统能量检测对独立本振相位噪声具有鲁棒性，并提出了无需估计的相位相干性合并方案以减轻相位噪声影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GRSM系统中，传统能量检测（ED）对独立本振相位噪声（ILO-PN）敏感，且受能量波动和噪声不确定性的影响，因此需要一种更鲁棒的检测方法。

Method: 采用直接RF采样获取接收信号样本，利用方向统计（DS）对信号相位建模；推导GLRT并简化为瑞利均匀性检验，获得闭式且与噪声方差无关的阈值；提出相位相干性感知合并方案以减轻ILO-PN。

Result: PUD对ILO-PN具有鲁棒性，对能量波动和噪声不确定性不敏感；所提出的相位相干性合并方案无需估计即可有效缓解ILO-PN。

Conclusion: PUD是一种有效的GRSM系统空间检测方案，在存在相位噪声的环境中优于传统能量检测。

Abstract: This paper introduces a phase-domain statistical detector, the Phase Uniformity Detector (PUD), for binary hypothesis testing in Generalized Receive Spatial Modulation (GRSM) systems. The PUD uses direct RF sampling to obtain received signal samples, their phases are modeled via Directional Statistics (DS). A Generalized Likelihood Ratio Test (GLRT) is derived and reduced to a Rayleigh uniformity test with a closed-form, noise-variance-independent threshold. Unlike conventional Energy Detection (ED), the PUD offers robust spatial detection under Independent Local Oscillator Phase Noise (ILO-PN), remaining insensitive to energy fluctuations and noise uncertainty. Additionally, a phase-coherence-aware combining scheme mitigates ILO-PN without requiring estimation.

</details>


### [100] [Learning to Compute on Dirty Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23252)
*Shreesal Shrestha,Kuranage Roche Rayan Ranasinghe,Giuseppe Thadeu Freitas de Abreu,Elza Erkip*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经网络的联合脏纸编码与空中计算框架，每个用户使用正弦激活的神经编码器实现非因果干扰预消除，联合神经解码器恢复所有用户消息，并在编解码网络收敛后利用多时隙块结构的神经AirComp估计器计算目标函数。这是首个完全基于学习的联合DPC干扰预消除与空中计算统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集成通信与计算（ICC）方法通常分别处理脏纸编码（DPC）和空中计算（AirComp），缺乏统一的端到端学习框架。本文旨在提出一种完全基于学习的方案，将DPC的干扰预消除与AirComp的目标函数计算有机结合。

Method: 1) 每个用户使用正弦激活的神经编码器，学习将自身的计算符号作为非因果已知干扰进行预消除，恢复与基于格型DPC方案一致的模周期性结构。2) 联合神经解码器从接收信号中恢复所有用户的消息。3) 在编解码网络收敛后，利用多时隙块结构的神经AirComp估计器估计计算符号的目标函数。

Result: 成功实现了DPC与AirComp的联合学习框架，编码器学习到了与格型DPC一致的模周期性结构，整体方案在统一框架下同时完成了干扰预消除和空中计算。

Conclusion: 这是首个完全基于学习的DPC干扰预消除与空中计算统一框架，为集成通信与计算提供了新的端到端学习范式。

Abstract: We propose a fully learning-based approach to integrated communication and computing (ICC) that combines dirty paper coding (DPC) with over-the-air computation. Each user employs a neural encoder with sinusoidal activations that learns to pre-cancel its own computing symbol as non-causally known interference, recovering modulo-like periodic structures consistent with lattice-based DPC schemes. A joint neural decoder recovers all users' messages from the received signal, while a separate neural AirComp estimator exploits a multi-slot block structure to estimate a target function of the computing symbols after the encoder-decoder network converges. To our knowledge, this is the first fully learning-based approach to jointly address DPC-based interference pre-cancellation and over-the-air computation in a unified framework.

</details>


### [101] [Symbol Rate-Code Rate Trade-offs for IM/DD 200G/400G per Lane LPO Transceivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23390)
*José Núñez-Kasaneva,Yunus Can Gültekin,Stefanos Dris,Paraskevas Bakopoulos,Nikos Argyris,Gabriele Liga*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文分析了带宽受限IM/DD系统中PAM-4/6/8调制格式的符号率与码率权衡，发现降低FEC码率可使高阶PAM在更低符号率下满足200G/400G吞吐量要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带宽受限的IM/DD系统中，针对LPO收发器，研究PAM-4/6/8调制格式下符号率与码率之间的权衡关系，以优化200G和400G每通道吞吐量性能。

Method: 以二进制对称信道的容量作为硬判决解码下的可达信息率性能指标，分析PAM-4/6/8调制格式在不同FEC码率下的符号率-码率权衡。

Result: 降低FEC码率低于KP4基线后，高阶PAM格式（PAM-6/8）与PAM-4相比，能在显著更低的符号率下满足吞吐量要求。

Conclusion: 在带宽受限IM/DD系统中，通过适度降低FEC码率，高阶PAM调制能够以更低符号率实现目标吞吐量，为LPO收发器设计提供了有效的权衡方案。

Abstract: We analyze the symbol rate-code rate trade-off in bandwidth-limited IM/DD systems targeting LPO transceivers, using PAM-4/6/8 as candidate modulation formats. We use the capacity of the binary symmetric channel as an achievable information rate under hard-decision decoding, serving as a performance metric for 200G and 400G per-lane throughput targets. Our results show that reducing the FEC code rate below the KP4 baseline allows higher-order PAM formats to operate at substantially lower symbol rates than PAM-4 while meeting the throughput requirement.

</details>


### [102] [Sensor-Stack Limits on Contactless In-Bed Body Position: A 20-Subject Multimodal Radar + Thermal LOSO Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23534)
*Dovy Paukstys*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了床边60GHz FMCW雷达与低分辨率热阵列传感器融合用于无接触式卧姿推断，发现传感器表征限制比分类器选择更具约束性。在20人便利样本的LOSO评估中，融合逻辑回归在床上/床下分类达到0.871平衡准确率，四类姿势堆叠集成达到0.674，但俯卧检测（召回率0.50，精确率0.41）尚不可部署。热阵列解决左右区分问题，但俯卧-仰卧呼吸信号仅在CFAR输出中呈现微弱类别级聚合效应（Cohen's d=0.61），且热阵列有效分辨率仅为标称一半。结论指向原始range-FFT访问而非CFAR检测上的分类器调优作为下一硬件实验方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 俯卧位监测与SUDEP（癫痫猝死）相关，但现有无接触式卧姿推断方法受限于传感器表征而非分类器选择。需要系统评估雷达与热阵列融合在便利样本上的实际性能边界，以指导硬件设计改进。

Method: 使用床边60GHz FMCW雷达（CFAR点云输出）和24×32标称分辨率热阵列，在20人便利样本（13名未成年人，年龄5-68岁，8个住所）上进行两个LOSO评估：273次监督卧姿保持（148.7分钟）和出入床检测。测试了逻辑回归、堆叠集成等管道，并进行了消融实验。

Result: 融合雷达+热阵列逻辑回归在床上/床下分类达0.871平衡准确率；四类姿势堆叠集成达0.674；俯卧召回率0.50、精确率0.41。热阵列解决左右区分（左右互换率从雷达的35-42%降至~8%），但俯卧-仰卧呼吸信号仅在CFAR输出中显现（Cohen's d=0.61），仅8.4%的保持有清晰峰。热阵列有效列数仅为标称的一半。

Conclusion: 当前传感器配置（CFAR点云+低分辨率热阵列）不足以实现可部署的俯卧检测。关键瓶颈在传感器表征层面，原始range-FFT访问应作为下一硬件实验方向，而非继续在CFAR检测结果上优化分类器。

Abstract: Contactless in-bed body-position inference can be limited by exposed sensor representation rather than classifier choice. We characterize a bedside 60 GHz frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radar with on-device constant-false-alarm-rate (CFAR) point-cloud output plus a low-resolution (24 x 32 nominal, rows x columns) thermal array on two leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) evaluations derived from the same 20-subject cohort: 273 supervised in-bed posture holds (148.7 minutes) and an enter/exit bed-presence audit from the same cohort. The cohort is a 20-subject friends-and-family calibration sample (13 minors, ages 5-68; 8 residences), so these are characterization figures on a convenience cohort, not population-level performance. The motivating use case is prone-position monitoring, because prone position has been associated with sudden unexpected death in epilepsy (SUDEP) in retrospective studies. Fused radar + thermal logistic regression reaches a 0.871 median leave-one-subject-out balanced accuracy for in-bed vs out-of-bed classification. For four-class posture, the best tested pipeline (a full-feature stacked ensemble) reaches 0.674 aggregate balanced accuracy. Prone recall is 0.50 and prone precision is 0.41, so this is not deployable prone detection. Ablations show that thermal solves left-vs-right discrimination (radar-only lateral swaps 35-42%; thermal-only ~8%), but the expected supine-vs-prone breathing cue appears only as a class-level aggregate shift in CFAR output (Cohen's d=0.61), with clean per-hold peaks in 8.4% of holds. The thermal array's usable resolution in this cache was half its nominal column count, too coarse to separate face-from-back-of-head signatures. The results point to raw range-FFT access, rather than classifier tuning on CFAR detections, as the next hardware experiment.

</details>


### [103] [High Speed High Signal-to-Noise Ratio Antenna Measurements -- Demonstration for UAV-Based Near-Field Measurements of Modulated Terrestrial Navigation Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23569)
*Thomas F. Eibert,Denis Unruh,Thomas Mittereder,Alexander H. Paulus*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过短时间采样、傅里叶变换、带通滤波和逆傅里叶变换来加速高信噪比天线测量的方法，并在仿真数据和无人机采集的DVOR及ILS近场测量数据上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统天线测量为获得高信噪比需要长积分时间，导致测量耗时过长，尤其当需要大量频率和空间采样点时问题更为突出。因此需要一种加速方法，在保持高信噪比的同时缩短测量时间。

Method: 1) 对每个采样点采用短测量时间采集数据；2) 对测量样本进行傅里叶变换；3) 用小带宽带通滤波器滤除噪声，提取所需信号分量；4) 通过逆傅里叶变换恢复高信噪比的测量样本。该方法适用于单频连续波信号和含有多个离散频率分量的周期性调制连续波信号。

Result: 通过仿真测试数据验证了方法的有效性，并将该方法成功应用于无人机采集的DVOR和ILS近场测量数据处理中。从提取的CW近场数据变换得到远场数据，并通过逆源求解获得了诊断信息。

Conclusion: 所提出的信号处理框架能在不牺牲信噪比的前提下显著缩短天线测量时间，适用于多种信号类型，并在实际航空导航系统近场测量中展示了良好的应用效果。

Abstract: Antenna measurements with high signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) require long measurement or integration times of the receiver and can, thus, lead to a very long duration of the measurements, especially if many frequency and spatial samples need to be collected. In order to speed up such measurements, an approach is presented, which collects all measurement samples with short measurement times, performs a Fourier transform of the measurement samples, bandpass filters the desired measurement signals with a small bandwidth, and obtains high-SNR measurement samples according to the short measurement times by inverse Fourier transform. This approach can be utilized with single-frequency continuous wave (CW) transmit signals, but also with transmit signals containing several discrete frequency components, as, e.g., found for periodically modulated CW carriers. The approach is first worked out and demonstrated for simulated test data. Next, it is utilized for the processing of modulated near-field (NF) measurement data collected via an uninhabited aerial vehicle (UAV) at a Doppler high-frequency omnidirectional radio range (DVOR) and at the localizer of an instrument landing system (ILS). The extracted CW NF data is transformed into the far field (FF) and diagnostic information is obtained from the underlying inverse source solutions.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.CO'></div>

# astro-ph.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Constraints on Horndeski Gravity with Phantom Crossing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20794)
*Krishna Naidoo,James Hallam,Tessa Baker,Sergi Sirera*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新的Horndeski标量-张量模型——渐近三次伽利略子（ACG）模型，该模型能够实现暗能量状态方程穿越w=-1（幻影分界线）。ACG模型可同时拟合Planck CMB、DESI BAO和DES超新星观测数据，并通过微扰观测（如星系-ISW互相关和空穴力剖面）严格约束参数空间。模型比较指标（χ²和贝叶斯证据）显示ACG和w₀wₐCDM均优于ΛCDM，且ACG为w₀wₐCDM行为提供了坚实的拉格朗日量基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期观测数据分析倾向于暗能量状态方程穿越w=-1的行为（幻影穿越），但现有理论模型在实现这一现象时面临挑战。本文旨在从第一性原理出发，构建一类能够自然产生幻影穿越且保持与物质最小耦合的Horndeski子类模型。

Method: 1. 提出Asymptotic Cubic Galileon (ACG)模型，作为Horndeski理论的新子类；2. 联合拟合Planck CMB、DESI BAO和DES超新星数据；3. 利用星系-ISW互相关和空穴力剖面等微扰观测进行参数约束；4. 采用χ²和贝叶斯证据进行模型比较。

Result: ACG模型能同时拟合多种宇宙学观测数据；微扰观测将可行的ACG模型约束在Horndeski参数空间的一个明确定义区域；χ²和贝叶斯证据表明ACG和w₀wₐCDM均优于ΛCDM，且ACG的拟合质量与w₀wₐCDM相当。

Conclusion: ACG模型为观测上偏好的w₀wₐCDM行为提供了坚实的拉格朗日量理论基础，超越了纯粹的现象学拟合，为在非线性尺度上进一步检验这些模型提供了动机。

Abstract: Gravity models in which the dark energy equation of state crosses $w=-1$, also known as the phantom divide, have received extensive interest due to recent analyses favouring this behaviour. We introduce a new subclass of Horndeski scalar-tensor models capable of generating phantom crossing, whilst remaining minimally coupled to matter: the Asymptotic Cubic Galileon (ACG) models. We show that ACG models can jointly fit the expansion history inferred from observations of the Planck cosmic microwave background, baryon acoustic oscillation measurements from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument, and distance-ladder supernovae measurements from the Dark Energy Survey. We then demonstrate that perturbative observables, including the galaxy-ISW cross-correlation and void force profile, provide powerful constraints that confine viable and testable ACG models to a well-defined region of the broader Horndeski landscape. Model comparison metrics, including $χ^{2}$ and Bayesian evidence, favour both ACG and $w_{0}w_{a}$CDM models over $Λ$CDM, with ACG providing a fit of comparable quality to $w_{0}w_{a}$CDM. Crucially, ACG models ground the observationally preferred $w_{0}w_{a}$CDM behaviour in a robust Lagrangian formulation. This enables interpretation beyond mere phenomenological fits, and motivates further tests of these models on nonlinear scales.

</details>


### [105] [Raising the reionization optical depth with inflationary CMB features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20795)
*Tanisha Jhaveri,Wayne Hu,Vivian Miranda*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 在ΛCDM宇宙学模型中，通过宇宙微波背景（CMB）各向异性测量确定再电离光学深度τ时，存在大角度偏振测量困难与温度低功率异常解释之间的不一致。若将低功率特征解释为暴胀的物理特征而非统计涨落，则τ值与BAO联合分析结果更为一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决ΛCDM框架下再电离光学深度τ的测量不确定性及不同数据源之间的不一致性，特别是大角度偏振测量与温度谱低功率异常之间的矛盾。

Method: 基于广义慢滚暴胀形式构建低功率特征模板，对Planck主数据及CMB+BAO联合数据分别进行边际化处理，比较不同解释下τ的95%置信限变化。

Result: 仅用Planck数据时τ上限为0.0696，加入BAO后下限增至0.074，产生矛盾。将低功率特征视为物理起源后，Planck上限升至0.075，CMB+BAO联合上限升至0.082，矛盾缓解。

Conclusion: 温度谱低功率特征若解释为暴胀物理特征，将显著提高再电离光学深度τ的允许上限，使其与BAO数据相容，但该分析本身不评估低功率特征的统计显著性。

Abstract: Within the highly successful $Λ$CDM paradigm established with cosmic microwave background (CMB) anisotropy measurements, the optical depth through reionization $τ$ is the most uncertain due both to the difficulty in measuring large-angle polarization and the assumptions made in their interpretation. Currently, for the Planck primary data in the flat $Λ$CDM cosmology with slow-roll inflation and standard reionization, the one-sided 95% upper limit for $τ$ is $τ_{\rm max}=0.0696$. Yet when all current CMB measurements excluding large-angle polarization are combined with baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) measurements, the one-sided 95% lower limit is an incompatible $τ_{\rm min}=0.074$. If the long-standing low-power feature of the temperature measurements is interpreted as physically originating from inflation then $τ$ inferred from large-angle polarization becomes larger. Marginalizing over templates of the low-power feature based on the generalized slow-roll formalism of inflation raises the Planck maximum to a more compatible $τ_{\rm max}=0.075$ which further increases to $τ_{\rm max} = 0.082$ with the inclusion of all CMB+BAO data. This marginalization does not assess the statistical significance of the low-power feature itself; rather, it shows that allowing a higher $τ$ is a consequence of interpreting the anomaly as a physical feature instead of a statistical fluctuation.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文利用LensMC对2025年3月发布的Euclid Quick Release 1图像进行了弱引力透镜分析，构建了首个剪切测量星表（63 deg²，星系面密度26-75 arcmin⁻²），通过经验偏差校正和两点统计/星团轮廓验证，成功将星系团质量约束延伸至z≈2、近10 Gyr演化历史。


<details>
