Motivation: 为Euclid Data Release 1提前准备并验证首个基于LensMC的剪切测量星表，确保弱引力透镜测量的系统误差可控，并利用Euclid的高分辨率与深度优势拓展对高红移星系团的质量约束能力。

Method: 使用LensMC对Euclid VIS图像进行星系形状、位置、权重等形态参数测量；基于星系大小和星等构建经验偏差校正；计算两点统计量和星团轮廓剖面，并与WISE和Dark Energy Survey的外部星团星表进行交叉验证；通过随机星团剪切堆叠分析评估残余系统误差。

Result: 在63 deg²范围内，I_E<24.5时星系面密度26 arcmin⁻²，I_E<27时达75 arcmin⁻²；未发现加性偏差的空间依赖性；成功将质量约10¹⁴M☉的星系团透镜轮廓约束延伸至z≈2，覆盖近10 Gyr演化历史；低红移大半径区域仍存在残余系统污染。

Conclusion: Euclid的图像分辨率与深度结合有效的系统误差控制，使得高红移星系团的弱引力透镜测量成为可能；经验偏差校正方法有效提升了测量精度，但低红移大半径bin仍需进一步改进系统误差处理。

Abstract: We present a LensMC lensing analysis of Euclid Quick Release 1 images that were made available in March 2025. We measured shapes, positions, weights, and other morphological parameters of galaxies with a surface number density of $26\;\text{arcmin}^{-2}$ for $I_{\scriptscriptstyle\rm E}<24.5$, achieving 75 arcmin$^{-2}$ for $I_{\scriptscriptstyle\rm E}<27$, in 63 deg$^2$ of Euclid VIS images. This is the first shear measurement catalogue produced with \lensmc in anticipation of the Euclid Data Release 1. To within the scope of this work and availability of survey volume, we found no spatial dependency in the additive biases. However, we developed an empirical bias correction based jointly on galaxy sizes and magnitudes, which was applied to the measurement of the lensing signal over the whole area. In order to validate the quality of our measurements, we calculated two-point statistics and cluster profile measurements, and cross-checked results with external cluster catalogues from WISE and the Dark Energy Survey. Additionally, by stacking shear profiles of random clusters we found that the low-redshift, large radii bins may be still contaminated by residual systematic effects. Thanks to Euclid image resolution and depth and overall good control of systematic errors, we are able to constrain the lensing profiles of clusters with masses of $10^{14}M_\odot$ out to $z\approx2$ over nearly 10 Gyr of evolution history.

</details>


### [107] [Loss-Cone-Limited Dark Matter Accretion onto Early Black Hole Seeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20964)
*Brian Zhang,Grant J. Mathews*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文开发了无碰撞暗物质被早期黑洞种子捕获的损失锥框架，发现普通恒星弛豫引起的暗物质驱动增长可忽略，原始黑洞或三轴轨道填充在足够致密的暗晕中可产生数量级增长，但自洽计算显示演化受供应限制，一旦低角动量相空间耗尽则捕获率崩溃，因此无碰撞暗物质捕获在普通NFW暗晕中难以解决早期超大质量黑洞增长问题，仅在稀有致密环境中可作为辐射暗的上包络通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高红移超大质量黑洞的快速出现挑战了传统重子物质吸积模型，促使研究者重新评估非重子物质（尤其是暗物质）作为黑洞增长替代通道的可能性。

Method: 结合Eddington反演NFW暗晕、相对论直接捕获边界和轨道平均的Fokker-Planck角动量输运处理，构建无碰撞暗物质捕获的损失锥框架。原始黑洞作为大质量扰动体，三轴或混沌近心轨道作为现象学上包络通道，并以TNG50校准的NFW基准进行测试。

Result: 普通恒星弛豫导致暗物质驱动增长可忽略；原始黑洞驱动的颗粒性在足够致密暗晕中可产生数量级增长；三轴或全损失锥供应可产生早期快速爆发。但自洽计算中演化受供应限制，低角动量相空间耗尽后捕获率崩溃，黑洞质量饱和。固定背景NFW计算会高估持续增长，TNG50校准基准下即使乐观填充也基本无增长。

Conclusion: 无碰撞暗物质捕获在普通NFW暗晕中难以解释早期超大质量黑洞增长，但在稀有致密环境中可作为辐射暗的上包络通道提供有限增长可能。

Abstract: The rapid appearance of supermassive black holes at high redshift motivates a reassessment of non-baryonic growth channels. We develop a loss-cone framework for collisionless dark-matter (DM) capture by early black-hole seeds, with particular attention to phase-space depletion and refilling. The calculation combines Eddington-inverted NFW-like halos, a relativistic direct-capture boundary, and an orbit-averaged Fokker-Planck treatment of angular-momentum transport. Primordial black holes (PBHs) are treated as massive perturbers whose refilling strength depends on both their abundance and individual mass. Collisionless refilling by triaxial or chaotic centrophilic orbits is included as a phenomenological upper-envelope channel. We show that ordinary stellar relaxation gives negligible DM-driven growth for the fiducial high-redshift seeds. PBH-driven granularity can yield order-of-magnitude growth in sufficiently compact halos, while triaxial or full-loss-cone supply can produce a rapid early burst. In the self-consistent calculations, however, the evolution generally becomes supply-limited. Once the accessible low-angular-momentum phase-space reservoir is depleted, the capture rate collapses and the black-hole mass saturates. Fixed-background NFW calculations therefore overestimate sustained growth, especially in the full-loss-cone limit. A TNG50-calibrated NFW benchmark gives negligible growth even under optimistic refilling assumptions. Collisionless DM capture is therefore unlikely to solve early SMBH growth in generic NFW-like halos, but it can provide a radiatively dark upper envelope in rare compact environments with efficient angular-momentum refilling.

</details>


### [108] [Accurate Galaxy Cluster Shear and Mass Calibration for LSST with AnaCal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21141)
*Conghao Zhou,Xiangchong Li,Hao-Yi Wu,Anja von der Linden,Tesla Jeltema,Tae-hyeon Shin,Simon Birrer,Tomomi Sunayama,Shenming Fu,Prakruth Adari,Lucie Baumont,Surhud More,Anthony Englert,Miranda Gorsuch,Andrés A. Plazas Malagón*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过图像模拟测试剪切估计器AnaCal在LSST十年数据条件下的星系团弱引力透镜性能，发现即使在中等剪切(|g|~0.15)下也能以极小偏差恢复输入剪切，并揭示了径向递减的平均剪切响应和中心区域的三阶正偏差，但最终团质量偏差仅为0.24±0.26%，证明AnaCal是LSST时代可靠的团质量校准工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系团丰度是精确宇宙学的重要途径，但团观测量和团质量之间的关系是关键挑战，而团弱引力透镜是建立该关系的标准工具。团透镜中心区域的剪切信号可进入非线性区域，许多剪切估计器依赖微扰假设，需明确验证其性能。

Method: 使用图像模拟，在代表10年LSST数据的条件下测试剪切估计器AnaCal的性能，分析其在非线性剪切区域的偏差特性，包括径向平均剪切响应的径向依赖性以及剪切估计的高阶偏差。

Result: AnaCal在|g|~0.15的中等剪切下能以极小偏差恢复输入剪切；发现径向递减的平均剪切响应（受收敛场径向依赖驱动）和团中心区域的剪切估计三阶正偏差；在理想条件下，团质量偏差为0.24±0.26%。

Conclusion: AnaCal是LSST era中实现精确团质量校准的稳健工具。

Abstract: The observed abundance of galaxy clusters as a function of mass and redshift provides a powerful route to precision cosmology; a key challenge for cluster cosmology is to establish the relation between cluster observables and cluster masses, for which cluster weak gravitational lensing has become the standard tool. A key challenge for cluster lensing is that the shear signal near cluster centers can reach the non-linear regime, where many shear estimators rely on perturbative assumptions that must be explicitly validated. In this work, we use image simulations to test the performance of the shear estimator AnaCal for cluster weak lensing under conditions representative of the 10-year LSST data. We find that AnaCal recovers the input shear with minimal bias even at mildly high shear, $|g|\sim 0.15$. We discover a radially decreasing mean shear response as seen previously in data, driven by the radial dependence of the convergence field; if unmodeled, this effect can bias shear inference. We also find a positive shear-estimation bias at third order in the reduced shear near the cluster center. However, because only a small fraction of galaxies lie in the high-shear regime and those measurements are further downweighted by the covariance matrix, the resulting mean cluster-mass bias for cluster lens masses in $[10^{14} M_\odot, 10^{15} M_\odot]$ -- adopting a scale cut of $\sim 0.2$ Mpc at $z=0.25$ -- is $0.24 \pm 0.26\%$ under ideal settings. These results demonstrate that AnaCal is a robust tool for accurate cluster mass calibration in the LSST era.

</details>


### [109] [Are Cosmological Data Excluding Sterile Neutrinos or Only the Fully Thermalized Limit?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21518)
*Artur Ladeira,Rafael C. Nunes,Eleonora Di Valentino,Stefano Gariazzo*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文对轻无菌中微子场景进行了宇宙学重新评估，发现当前观测并不普遍排除无菌中微子，而是主要对完全热化或高占据场景施加强约束。完全热化场景（FTS）在标准数据组合下被强烈排斥，但加入局域H0先验后可与标准ΛCDM竞争；而部分占据场景（如Dodelson-Widrow型）仍然可行。约束的关键在于有效无菌丰度而非物理质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有宇宙学观测是否真正排除了无菌中微子作为一类粒子，还是仅约束了完全热化的特殊情况？不同产生机制和相空间分布下的无菌中微子场景尚未被系统性对比评估。

Method: 考虑三种无菌中微子实现：完全热化（FTS）、温度抑制热 relics（DTS）、Dodelson-Widrow型（DW）。在ΛCDM和CPL动态暗能量框架下，结合Planck CMB、DESI DR2 BAO、PantheonPlus和Union3超新星数据，进行宇宙学参数约束。

Result: FTS在无局域H0先验时被强烈排斥；加入H0^DN先验后可与ΛCDM竞争但质量仍与零一致。DW场景与观测广泛兼容，DTS场景所受宇宙学压力最弱。约束主要来自有效无菌丰度m_eff^sterile，而非物理质量。

Conclusion: 当前宇宙学观测并未普遍排除无菌中微子，而是对完全热化或高占据场景施加强约束，强调在生产历史和相空间分布对解释宇宙学约束时的重要性。

Abstract: We present a cosmological reassessment of light sterile-neutrino scenarios, examining whether current observations exclude sterile neutrinos as a class or primarily constrain the fully thermalized case. We consider three distinct realizations: (i) a fully thermalized sterile species (FTS), (ii) a thermal relic with a suppressed temperature relative to the active neutrino background (DTS), and (iii) a Dodelson-Widrow-like (DW) sterile neutrino with reduced phase-space normalization. Constraints are derived within both LambdaCDM and the CPL dynamical dark-energy framework using combinations of Planck CMB data, DESI DR2 BAO measurements, and the PantheonPlus and Union3 Type Ia supernova samples. For baseline data combinations without a local H0 prior, the FTS scenario is strongly disfavored in both cosmological models. Adding the local H0^DN prior allows LambdaCDM+FTS to accommodate the high local H0 value and become statistically competitive with standard LambdaCDM once SNIa data are included, although the sterile-neutrino mass remains consistent with zero. By contrast, partially populated sterile-neutrino scenarios remain viable: the DW realization is broadly compatible with current observations, while the DTS scenario yields the weakest cosmological pressure among the cases considered. Overall, cosmological data mainly require a strongly suppressed effective sterile abundance, leading to tight constraints on m_eff^sterile while allowing substantially weaker bounds on the physical sterile mass. We conclude that current observations do not generically exclude sterile neutrinos, but rather place strong pressure on fully thermalized or highly populated scenarios, highlighting the importance of production history and phase-space distribution when interpreting cosmological constraints.

</details>


### [110] [Wave-optics imprints of dark matter subhalos on strongly lensed gravitational waves. II. Saddle images and detectability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21519)
*Shin'ichiro Ando*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了强引力透镜引力波中波光学干涉对暗物质次结构的新探测方法，将计算扩展到鞍点像，并评估了LISA的探测能力。在冷暗物质次结构蒙特卡罗模拟中，次结构引起百分比的振幅和相位调制，且最小像与鞍点像的平均放大率出现分裂。匹配滤波分析结合透镜参数投影后，62%的模拟可实现5σ以上联合探测，预计LISA任务期间可探测10-20个次结构信号。这为电磁观测无法触及的$10^{4}$-$10^{7}\,M_\odot$质量尺度次结构提供了灵敏的互补探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波光学干涉效应为探测引力透镜中的暗物质次结构提供了新途径，但此前仅计算了最小放大像的信号。需要将理论推广到鞍点像以全面评估信号特征，并验证LISA是否具备可探测性。

Method: 提出时域方法解决鞍点处放大因子的数值计算难题（因等到达时间轮廓开放且次结构信号为大项间小差）；采用蒙特卡罗模拟冷暗物质次结构系综；使用匹配滤波分析投影透镜参数后提取次结构信号。

Result: 次结构在两种像奇偶性下均引起百分比级振幅和相位调制，但最小像净放大、鞍点像净缩小。联合探测灵敏度达5σ以上的概率为62%（源位于透镜焦散附近、冲击参数$y_{\rm src}\lesssim0.1$时），预计LISA任务期内可探测10-20个次结构事件。

Conclusion: 强引力透镜引力波是探测$10^{4}$-$10^{7}\,M_\odot$质量尺度暗物质次结构的灵敏且互补的新探针，填补了电磁观测在该质量区间的空白。

Abstract: Wave-optics interference in strongly lensed gravitational waves is a new interferometric probe of dark matter substructure: a subhalo population threading a galaxy-scale lens imprints frequency-dependent distortions on the amplification factor of each macro image. In a companion paper (arXiv:2603.04267), we computed these imprints for the magnified minimum image. Here, we extend the calculation to the saddle-point image and we assess the detectability of the combined signal with the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA). Evaluating the amplification factor at a saddle is numerically delicate, because the equal-arrival-time contours are open and the subhalo signal is a small difference of large terms; we present a time-domain method that resolves it. Across a Monte Carlo ensemble of cold dark matter subhalo realizations, subhalos induce percent-level amplitude and phase modulations in both image parities, while the mean (de)magnification splits by parity: the minimum is net magnified and the saddle net demagnified. Demodulating the macro-image interference recovers the per-image modulations, and a matched-filter analysis that projects out the lens parameters yields a combined detection above $5σ$ in $62\%$ of realizations for fiducial massive-black-hole-binary sources of total mass $\sim10^{6}\,M_\odot$ at redshift $1.5$, provided the source lies close to the lens caustic at small impact parameter $y_{\rm src}\lesssim0.1$. Folding these naive per-event significances through optimistic strong-lensing rate forecasts yields $10$-$20$ substructure detections over the LISA mission. Strongly lensed gravitational waves are thus a sensitive, complementary probe of substructure at $10^{4}$-$10^{7}\,M_\odot$ scales inaccessible to electromagnetic observations.

</details>


### [111] [Dark Energy in the DESI Era: A Brief Review of Evidence, Beyond-$Λ$CDM Interpretations, and Tensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21826)
*Tian-Nuo Li,Guo-Hong Du,Hao Wang,Yun-He Li,Jing-Fei Zhang,Xin Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: DESI的重子声学振荡测量表明宇宙晚期膨胀历史可能偏离ΛCDM模型，呈现动态暗能量（跨越幻影分界线的quintom行为）的迹象。本文综述了这些迹象对超越ΛCDM物理的可能含义，讨论了参数化形式、数据集组合、残留系统误差和内部张力对解释偏好的影响，以及不同新物理场景（相互作用暗能量、非最小耦合引力、非标准暗物质）在背景层面的等效性，并分析了这些解释对H₀、S₈和中微子质量之和等宇宙学张力的不同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESI的最新重子声学振荡测量对标准ΛCDM模型在描述晚期宇宙膨胀历史方面是否足够充分提出了质疑，需要系统评估这些迹象的可靠性及其对超越ΛCDM的新物理的可能含义。

Method: 综述性分析，综合DESI的BAO测量数据、宇宙微波背景和Ia型超新星数据，考察不同参数化形式和数据集组合下对动态暗能量偏好的强度变化，对比多种超越ΛCDM的新物理场景在背景演化层面的等效性，并分析这些场景对H₀、S₈和中微子质量之和等当前宇宙学张力的不同影响。

Result: DESI数据显示与ΛCDM模型的偏离，表现为暗能量状态方程跨越w=-1的quintom行为；这种偏好强度依赖于参数化形式和数据集组合；多种新物理机制（相互作用暗能量、非最小耦合引力、非标准暗物质）可在背景层面产生等效的膨胀历史；不同新物理解释对H₀、S₈和中微子质量之和等宇宙学张力有不同影响。

Conclusion: DESI提出的问题不仅是暗能量是否随时间演化，更关键的是如何在精确宇宙学框架下从系统误差中区分出真正的新物理场景。

Abstract: Recent baryon acoustic oscillation measurements from DESI provide important new clues for reassessing whether the standard $Λ$CDM model offers a sufficient description of the late-time expansion history of the Universe. When combined with cosmic microwave background and type Ia supernova data, these measurements show an apparent departure from the $Λ$CDM model, commonly described as dynamical dark energy (DDE) with equation of state crossing the phantom divide (i.e., quintom behavior). This review examines the current status of the DESI-motivated indications for DDE and their possible implications for physics beyond $Λ$CDM. We discuss how the strength of the preference for DDE depends on the adopted parametrization and dataset combination, and how residual systematics or internal tensions among datasets may affect its interpretation. At the background level, several mechanisms beyond $Λ$CDM can produce similar expansion histories. We therefore further discuss how the same effective departure from $w=-1$ may arise from physically distinct scenarios, including interacting dark energy, non-minimally coupled gravity, and non-standard dark matter. Meanwhile, these different new-physics interpretations may have different implications for current cosmological tensions, especially those involving $H_0$, $S_8$, and $\sum m_ν$. In conclusion, the question posed by DESI is not merely whether dark energy evolves with time, but rather how, within the framework of precision cosmology, to disentangle new physics scenarios from systematic errors.

</details>


### [112] [Optimization of Tessellation-based Statistics: Void Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21864)
*Yu Liu,Cheng Zhao,Yu Yu,Zhejie Ding,Jean-Paul Kneib*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文识别了基于三角剖分法的宇宙大尺度结构统计量对点位扰动高度敏感、引入额外统计误差的问题，提出了一种通过子采样和平均化来增强稳定性的测量方案。以Delaunay和Voronoi两种空洞统计（空洞尺寸函数、两点相关函数、功率谱）为例，证明了该方法能显著消除三角剖分不稳定性导致的误差，大幅提升重子声学振荡的信噪比及宇宙学参数约束能力，具有简单、高效、广泛适用性的特点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三角剖分法在大尺度结构分析中广泛应用，但其对点密度和位置扰动高度敏感，容易导致三角剖分构型的显著重组，进而在各类基于三角剖分的统计量中引入大量额外统计误差，削弱了宇宙学约束能力。本文旨在识别并解决这一问题。

Method: 提出一种通过子采样（subsampling）和平均化（averaging）来增强三角剖分统计量稳定性的测量方案。以Delaunay和Voronoi三角剖分为基础，测量两类空洞的多种原初空洞统计量（空洞尺寸函数、空洞两点相关函数、空洞功率谱），并通过严格测试验证方法有效性。

Result: 发现空洞统计中的统计不确定性主要归因于三角剖分不稳定性；所提方法能大幅消除这些散射，显著提升空洞重子声学振荡的信噪比，并明显改善空洞统计对宇宙学参数的约束能力。

Conclusion: 该方法简单、高效且具有广泛适用性，有望发展成为未来测量基于三角剖分统计量的标准框架，从而最大化从星系巡天中提取宇宙学信息的能力。

Abstract: Tessellation methods are extensively employed in the analyses of cosmic large-scale structure (LSS). However, these techniques are highly sensitive to perturbations in both densities and positions of points, often leading to substantial rearrangements of tessellation configurations. As a result, considerable additional statistical errors are introduced in various tessellation-based statistics, thereby weakening their cosmological constraints. In this work, we identify this issue and propose an efficacious measurement scheme through subsampling and averaging to enhance the stabilities of tessellation-based statistics. As a case study, we apply the new scheme to measure multiple primary void statistics [i.e., void size function (VSF), void two-point correlation function (VTCF), and void power spectrum (VPS)] in two distinct classes of voids, based on Delaunay and Voronoi tessellations, respectively. We notice that the statistical uncertainties in void statistics can be predominantly attributed to tessellation instabilities. Through rigorous testing, we demonstrate that the proposed method can substantially eliminate these scatters to deeply mine the statistical power of void statistics. Specifically, we find that our method can dramatically boost the signal-to-noise ratios (SNRs) of void Baryon Acoustic Oscillations (BAOs) and significantly improve the constraining power of void statistics on cosmological parameters. These findings showcase enormous application potentials of our new method in maximizing extraction of cosmological information from galaxy surveys. Importantly, our method is simple yet highly potent with broad applicability, hopefully evolving into a standard framework for measuring tessellation-based statistics in the future.

</details>


### [113] [Phantom-Divide Crossing in Exponentially Coupled Quintessence and the Role of Neutrino-Mass Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22023)
*Jincheng Wang,Hongwei Yu,Puxun Wu*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了具有指数势和指数耦合到冷暗物质的Quintessence暗能量模型（CQ-EXP模型），利用Planck CMB、DESI BAO和DES-Dovekie超新星观测数据进行分析，并考察了中微子质量变化对约束的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Quintessence暗能量与冷暗物质之间的耦合是否被观测数据所支持，以及中微子质量自由度如何影响耦合信号的统计显著性，从而检验标准ΛCDM模型之外的新物理。

Method: 采用CQ-EXP模型（指数势+指数耦合），结合Planck CMB、DESI BAO和DES-Dovekie超新星三类观测数据，分别固定中微子质量总和（∑m_ν=0.06 eV）和释放有效中微子质量参数（∑m_{ν,eff}）两种情形进行贝叶斯统计分析，并与w0waCDM模型对比。

Result: 固定∑m_ν时，耦合参数β偏离零超过3σ，数据偏好β<0分支（有效暗能量EoS穿越w=-1的phantom分界线）；释放∑m_{ν,eff}时，数据偏好负值，耦合偏好减弱，β>0与β<0两种情形χ²_min几乎相同，均略高于w0waCDM模型，无法统计区分。

Conclusion: 固定中微子质量时，当前观测支持具有phantom分界线穿越的CQ-EXP模型；允许负有效中微子质量时，无穿越的CQ-EXP模型也能有效解释最新观测数据，表明中微子质量自由度与暗能量-暗物质耦合之间存在简并。

Abstract: We investigate a quintessence dark-energy model with an exponential potential and an exponential coupling to cold dark matter (CDM), hereafter referred to as the CQ-EXP model, using Planck CMB, DESI BAO, and DES-Dovekie supernova observations. We also examine how variations in the neutrino mass sector affect the constraints. When the neutrino mass sum is fixed at $\sum m_ν=0.06$ eV, the data favor a coupling between quintessence and CDM, with the coupling parameter $β$ deviating from zero at more than $3σ$. In particular, the observations favor the $β<0$ branch, where the energy transfer between the two dark sectors changes sign and the effective equation of state (EoS) of dark energy crosses the phantom divide, $w=-1$. When the effective neutrino mass parameter $\sum m_{ν,\mathrm{eff}}$ is treated as a free parameter, the data show a preference for negative values of $\sum m_{ν,\mathrm{eff}}$. This additional freedom weakens the preference for the coupling between quintessence and CDM and leads to nearly identical values of $χ^2_{\rm min}$ for the CQ-EXP models with $β>0$ and $β<0$, corresponding respectively to models without and with phantom-divide crossing in the effective EoS. Both values are slightly larger than that obtained in the $w_0w_a$CDM model, indicating that the CQ-EXP model cannot be statistically distinguished from the $w_0w_a$CDM model with the data considered here. Therefore, when $\sum m_ν$ is fixed, current observations favor the CQ-EXP model with phantom-divide crossing. In contrast, when negative values of $\sum m_{ν,\mathrm{eff}}$ are allowed, a CQ-EXP dark energy without crossing $w=-1$ can also provide an effective explanation of the latest observations.

</details>


### [114] [Dark Matter as an Inflationary Relic in Warm Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22147)
*Swagat S. Mishra,Umang Kumar,Suratna Das,Varun Sahni*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文发现温暴涨结束后，耗散比率Q迅速下降会导致残余暴胀子凝聚体，该残余物在二次势极小值下可作为冷暗物质存在，质量约为0.02 MeV，更大的质量会导致宇宙过闭合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为温暴涨会通过耗散将暴胀子能量完全转化为辐射，本文试图检验这一预期是否总是成立，并探索残余暴胀子作为暗物质的可能性。

Method: 在强耗散温暴涨场景中，分析了耗散比率Q=Υ/(3H)在暴涨结束后快速下降的动力学过程，采用最小可重整化势和Υ∝T³的耗散系数，结合当前宇宙学数据对暴胀子质量进行约束。

Result: 强耗散条件下，暴涨结束后耗散比率迅速下降，抑制了进一步的能量转移，导致残余暴胀子凝聚体。该残余物作为冷暗物质，观测丰度要求其质量约为0.02 MeV，更大质量会导致宇宙过闭合。

Conclusion: 残余暴胀子暗物质机制将温暴涨后的动力学转化为对参数空间的新晚期约束，且物质主导标度演化在BBN之前完成，避免了长寿命暴胀子暗辐射成分。

Abstract: Warm inflation is usually expected to completely deplete the inflaton condensate by dissipating its energy into radiation. We show that this expectation fails in a simple and observationally viable regime. In a strongly dissipative warm inflationary scenario, the dissipative ratio, $Q=Υ/(3H)$, can fall rapidly after the end of inflation as the system approaches radiation domination, thereby suppressing further energy transfer to the thermal bath. This leads to a residual inflaton condensate, which subsequently evolves as an effectively non-dissipative scalar field. For potentials with a stable quadratic minimum, this remnant inflaton manifests as a cold dark matter component. We establish this mechanism for the minimal renormalizable potential, with a dissipative coefficient $Υ\propto T^3$. In this case, current cosmological data allow strong dissipation while leaving the inflaton mass weakly constrained by inflationary observables. The observed dark matter abundance then fixes its mass to be $m \approx 0.02\,{\rm MeV}$, while larger masses overclose the Universe. The transition to matter-like scaling occurs well before BBN, avoiding a long-lived inflaton dark radiation component. Relic inflaton dark matter therefore turns the post-inflationary dynamics of warm inflation into a new late time constraint on its parameter space.

</details>


### [115] [The local ultraviolet signature of Type Ia supernova environments from HST and MUSE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22173)
*Lluís Galbany,Cullen Abelson,Alaa Alburai,Joseph P Anderson,Yago Ascasibar,Chris Ashall,Carles Badenes,Chris Burns,Èlia Diéguez Gurnés,Albert García Soto,Claudia P. Gutiérrez,Eric Y. Hsiao,Hanindyo Kuncarayakti,Andrew J. Levan,Jospeh Lyman,Mark M. Phillips,Sebastian F. Sánchez,Ramon Sanfeliu,Maximilian Stritzinger,Danny Steeghs*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 基于340颗低红移Ia型超新星（SN Ia）的HST/WFC3近紫外成像与VLT/MUSE光学积分场光谱联合数据，研究发现紫外数据能有效打破年龄-尘埃-金属丰度简并，且局域环境性质与SN Ia光变曲线拉伸参数x1存在强相关性（>6.8σ），但经标准光变曲线校正后哈勃残差对环境依赖较弱（~0.04-0.06 mag），支持年龄-拉伸主导机制已被标准校正吸收，残留环境效应微弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究中，SN Ia局域环境对光变曲线性质及标准化光度的影响尚不明确，且近紫外成像与光学积分场光谱的联合应用在此领域较为缺乏，因此需要系统性探究UV+光学数据如何更好地表征SN Ia环境并揭示其与超新星观测量的联系。

Method: 从340颗低红移SN Ia遗址获取HST/WFC3 F275W近紫外成像和VLT/MUSE匹配光谱数据，测量局域测光、提取1 kpc孔径光谱、采用UV+光学联合拟合建模恒星连续谱，推导局域恒星和气体相性质，并用SALT3-NIR拟合SN光变曲线。

Result: 235/337个有效站点检测到UV发射；UV与Hα面星形成率强相关但UV估计系统偏高0.5 dex；引入UV约束使光度加权年龄中位数增加0.20 dex（UV探测）和0.45 dex（UV未探测）；F275W-r与联合拟合恒星年龄强相关；联合恒星年龄、Hα比星形成率、UV比星形成率均与SALT3拉伸参数x1强相关（>6.8σ）；标准化后哈勃残差趋势较弱（~0.04-0.06 mag）。

Conclusion: 研究支持年龄-拉伸作为主要机制，该效应已被标准光变曲线校正基本吸收；局域环境对标准化后亮度的残留依赖较弱且微妙，但UV数据对于打破参数简并、改善环境表征具有重要意义。

Abstract: (Abridged.) The local environment of a Type Ia supernova (SN Ia) encodes information on progenitor age, star formation, dust, and chemical enrichment that may influence both its light-curve properties and its standardized luminosity. We assemble a homogeneous explosion-site dataset to test how near-ultraviolet (UV) imaging combined with optical integral-field spectroscopy improves the characterization of SN Ia environments and their connection to SN observables. We analyze 340 low-redshift SN Ia sites observed with HST/WFC3 F275W and matched VLT/MUSE spectroscopy. For each site we measure local photometry, extract a 1 kpc-aperture spectrum, model the stellar continuum with a joint UV+optical fit, derive local stellar and gas-phase properties, and fit the SN light curves with SALT3-NIR. UV emission is detected at 235 of the 337 sites with valid HST imaging. The UV- and Halpha-based SFR surface densities are strongly correlated, with the UV estimates systematically higher by about 0.5 dex. Including the UV constraint shifts local luminosity-weighted ages toward older values by a median of +0.20 dex for UV detections and +0.45 dex for UV non-detections, showing that UV data help break age-dust-metallicity degeneracies. Consistently, F275W-r is very strongly correlated with the joint-fit stellar age. Within the 169-SN Ia calibration sample, the joint stellar age, the Halpha specific SFR, and the UV specific SFR all correlate strongly with the SALT3 stretch parameter x1, with significances above 6.8 sigma. After standardization, Hubble-residual trends are weaker, with amplitudes of about 0.04-0.06 mag. The results support a primary age-stretch mechanism largely absorbed by standard light-curve corrections, while any residual luminosity dependence on local environment is weaker and subtler.

</details>


### [116] [Revisiting cosmic anisotropy with the Pantheon+ compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22302)
*Yong Zhou,Dong Zhao*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用偶极子拟合（DF）和半球比较（HC）两种方法，基于更新的Pantheon+样本研究宇宙各向异性。结果显示，全样本中偶极信号统计上较弱，但低红移子样本及特定调查子集（5, 56, 63, 150）表现出约2σ显著性的偶极信号；HC方法下全样本最大各向异性水平显著性为1.56σ，方向主要由SNLS子样本决定。这些结果暗示观测到的各向异性可能源于局部结构或数据分布不均匀，而非内在宇宙各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究宇宙学原理中宇宙各向同性的基本假设是否成立，利用更丰富的数据样本（Pantheon+）和两种互补方法（DF和HC）系统检验宇宙各向异性信号。

Method: 采用偶极子拟合（DF）和半球比较（HC）两种方法，对完整的Pantheon+样本及其不同红移子样本（低-z、中-z、高-z）和特定调查子集进行分析。

Result: 全样本DF方法偶极信号弱；低-z子样本偶极振幅A_D=0.952×10^{-3}（~2σ），指向(149.77°, -12.20°)；调查5、56、63、150组合子集振幅A_D=1.730×10^{-3}，指向(153.05°, -1.25°)。HC方法全样本AL_max=0.289±0.052（1.56σ），指向(127.97°, 17.90°)，主要由SNLS子样本驱动。

Conclusion: 观测到的各向异性信号依赖于所选子样本，更可能源于局部结构或数据集分布不均匀，而非真实的宇宙本征各向异性，这支持了宇宙学原理的基本假设。

Abstract: We investigate cosmic anisotropy within the updated Pantheon+ sample using both the dipole fitting (DF) and hemisphere comparison (HC) methods. With the DF method, the dipole signal within the full sample is statistically weak. However, the low-$z$ subsample yields a dipole signal of $A_{\mathrm{D}} = 0.952^{+0.454}_{-0.403} \times 10^{-3}$ at $\sim 2σ$ significance, pointing towards $(l,b) = (149.77^\circ, -12.20^\circ)$. This signal is predominantly driven by a combined subset of surveys 5, 56, 63, and 150, which is characterized by an amplitude of $A_{\mathrm{D}} = 1.730_{-0.715}^{+0.554} \times 10^{-3}$ towards $(l,b) = (153.05^\circ, -1.25^\circ)$. For the HC method, the full sample yields a maximum anisotropy level of $\mathrm{AL}_{\mathrm{max}} = 0.289 \pm 0.052$ oriented towards $(l,b) = (127.97^\circ, 17.90^\circ)$ with a $1.56σ$ significance. This preferred direction is primarily determined by the highly inhomogeneous SNLS subsample, whereas the low-$z$ and high-$z$ subsamples act to suppress the anisotropy level along this axis. These subsample-dependent results suggest that the apparent anisotropy arises from local structures or the inhomogeneous distribution of the datasets rather than an intrinsic cosmic anisotropy.

</details>


### [117] [Fast Radio Burst Cosmology: Hubble Tension and Dark Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22390)
*Fayin Wang,Xuandong Jia,Daohong Gao,Zigao Dai*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了快速射电暴（FRB）作为宇宙学探针的应用，重点阐述其在测量哈勃常数、限制暗能量性质方面的优势与进展，并讨论了当前精度限制因素及未来前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FRB作为亮度高、持续时间短的河外射电瞬变源，能够通过精确的色散-红移关系独立测量低红移宇宙膨胀率，弥补Ia型超新星、重子声学振荡和宇宙微波背景辐射等传统探针的不足，提供互补的宇宙学约束。

Method: 利用局域化FRB的精确色散度量-红移关系，建模视线方向上星际介质的积分电子密度，从而独立测量哈勃常数；利用局域化与非局域化FRB样本约束暗能量状态方程参数。

Result: 当前局域化与非局域化FRB样本已能提供具有竞争力的H₀约束，并为暗能量状态方程参数提供有效限制。

Conclusion: 随着高精度FRB巡天和局域化样本的快速增长，FRB有望对晚期宇宙加速膨胀、暗能量演化以及宇宙重子分布提供严格约束。

Abstract: Fast radio bursts (FRBs) are luminous, millisecond-duration extragalactic radio transients that have emerged as a powerful, complementary cosmological probe for investigating the late-time cosmic evolution, offering unique advantages over conventional probes such as Type Ia supernovae, baryon acoustic oscillations, and cosmic microwave background radiation. This review systematically summarizes the cosmological applications of FRBs, focusing on their critical roles in measuring the Hubble constant ($H_0$) and constraining dark energy properties. Benefiting from the precise dispersion measure (DM) - redshift relation of localized FRBs, the integrated electron density of the intergalactic medium (IGM) along the line of sight can be tightly modeled, enabling independent and low-redshift measurements of the cosmic expansion rate. Current FRB samples consisting of localized and non-localized events provide competitive $H_0$ constraints, offering an independent method to measure $H_0$. FRBs also serve as effective tracers to constrain dark energy equation-of-state parameters. We comprehensively discuss key limiting factors for FRB cosmological precision, including uncertainties in Galactic and host galaxy electron density models, and IGM inhomogeneities. With the rapid growth of high-precision FRB surveys and localized FRB samples, FRBs are promising to provide stringent constraints on late-time cosmic acceleration, dark energy evolution and cosmic baryons.

</details>


### [118] [Shape-Preserving Evolution of the Global Ultraviolet Quasar Luminosity Function to $z\simeq7.5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22415)
*Wenjie Wang,Zunli Yuan,Yu Luo,Longhua Qin*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文基于70,960颗I类类星体的均匀样本，在0.1≤z≤7.5红移范围内对紫外类星体光度函数进行全局无偏似然分析。通过测试保持形状不变的光度和密度演化（LADE）框架，发现该框架能统计高效且紧凑地描述全球紫外QLF，其中光度演化成分在z≈2-3快速上升后趋于平缓，密度演化成分则在高红移持续下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更精确地描述类星体光度函数随红移的演化规律，克服传统双幂律模型在描述高红移演化时的局限性，寻找统计高效且物理意义清晰的参数化描述框架。

Method: 采用保持局部LF形状不变的LADE框架，构建81种光度和密度演化函数组合模型（基于双幂律局部LF），与两个灵活双幂律参考模型对比，并通过修正Schechter函数验证结果的稳健性。使用AIC和BIC作为模型选择准则。

Result: 基准LADE模型AIC和BIC最低；12种基于DPL的LADE模型优于两个FDPL参考模型；修正Schechter函数得到相同的最优演化结构；光度演化在z≈2-3达到峰值后趋于平稳，密度演化在高红移持续下降。

Conclusion: 形状保持的LADE框架能统计高效且紧凑地描述全球紫外QLF的演化，为类星体光度函数的经验建模提供了更优的选择。

Abstract: We present a global unbinned-likelihood analysis of the rest-frame ultraviolet (UV) quasar luminosity function (QLF) over $0.1\le z\le7.5$, using a final analysis sample of 70,960 Type~1 quasars selected from the homogenized compilation of Kulkarni et al. (2019). We test a shape-preserving luminosity and density evolution (LADE) framework, in which the local luminosity function (LF) shape is fixed and the redshift evolution is described by separate density- and luminosity-evolution factors. For a double-power-law (DPL) local LF, we search 81 LADE models built from nine density-evolution and nine luminosity-evolution functions, and compare them with two flexible double-power-law (FDPL) reference models. The fiducial shape-preserving LADE model gives the lowest Akaike information criterion (AIC) and Bayesian information criterion (BIC) values among the main models considered, and 12 DPL-based LADE models have lower AIC and BIC values than both FDPL reference models. Repeating the model-grid analysis with a modified-Schechter local LF gives the same preferred evolutionary structure, indicating that the result is not driven only by the assumed local LF shape. The fitted evolution functions further show that the luminosity-evolution component is the more stable part of the LADE decomposition: it rises rapidly to $z\simeq2$--3 and then flattens or slowly declines. The density-evolution component is more model dependent, but the preferred LADE models consistently require a declining effective normalization toward high redshift. Taken together, we conclude that a shape-preserving framework offers a statistically efficient and compact empirical description of the global UV QLF.

</details>


### [119] [Tracing Large-scale Structure with the MeerKLASS On-the-Fly Survey: Angular Clustering of Radio Sources at 816 MHz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22432)
*Sourabh Paul,Suman Chatterjee,Keith Grainge,Laura Wolz,Aishrila Mazumder,Steven Cunnington,Sarvesh Mangla,Joseph J. Mohr,Mario G. Santos,Oleg Smirnov,Cyril Tasse*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文报道了MeerKLASS UHF连续谱数据发布1（DR1）中射电源角两点相关函数w(θ)的首次测量。在DESI覆盖区域内~800平方度、参考频率816 MHz的观测中，探测到0.02°至10°范围内的正聚类信号。通过固定斜率幂律建模和ΛCDM投影物质模板拟合，获得了有效大尺度偏置因子b_eff=1.998±0.350（AGN先验）和1.530±0.265（TOTAL先验），并反演出相关长度r0。结果表明N(z)是主导建模不确定性来源，MeerKLASS UHF DR1成为中频射电连续谱聚类研究的新宽场数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次利用MeerKLASS UHF on-the-fly连续谱数据发布1（DR1）测量射电源的角两点相关函数，填补中频（816 MHz）宽场射电聚类测量的空白，并为后续利用更大样本和光学/红外光谱改进红移标定、实现种群分裂聚类和偏置演化测量奠定基础。

Method: 使用MeerKLASS UHF OTF连续谱DR1的Stokes-I成像数据（~800 deg²，816 MHz），测量射电源的角两点相关函数w(θ)；采用固定斜率（γ=1.8）幂律w(θ)=A θ^(1-γ)建模中间尺度信号；利用CAMB和Limber投影计算ΛCDM投影物质模板w_DM(θ)，并引入积分约束修正，通过拟合得到有效大尺度偏置b_eff；采用两种T-RECS红移分布先验（AGN和TOTAL）；通过Limber反演幂律振幅获得相关长度r0。

Result: 在0.02°≤θ≤10°范围内探测到正聚类信号；固定斜率幂律拟合得到A(1°)=(1.434±0.475)×10^{-3}，log10A=-2.843^{+0.124}_{-0.175}；AGN先验下b_eff=1.998±0.350，TOTAL先验下b_eff=1.530±0.265；对应r0分别为6.18±1.13和5.59±1.02 h^{-1}Mpc；N(z)不确定性是建模的主要误差来源。

Conclusion: MeerKLASS UHF DR1被确立为新的宽场中频射电连续谱聚类数据集；当前结果受限于红移分布先验的建模不确定性；未来随着MeerKLASS数据扩展和光学/IR光谱红移标定改进，将能实现种群分裂聚类和偏置演化测量，显著降低建模不确定性。

Abstract: We present the first measurement of the angular two-point correlation function \(w(θ)\) of radio sources from the MeerKAT Large Area Synoptic Survey (MeerKLASS) UHF on-the-fly (OTF) continuum Data Release~1. DR1 provides interferometric Stokes-\(I\) imaging at a reference frequency of 816\,MHz over \(\sim 800~\mathrm{deg}^2\) within the DESI footprint. We detect a positive clustering signal over \(0.02^\circ \lesssim θ\lesssim 10^\circ\). The measurement is stable to reasonable variations of the depth mask and flux threshold on intermediate and large scales. Modelling the intermediate-scale signal (\(0.112^\circ\leθ\le 1.36^\circ\)) with a fixed-slope power law (\(γ=1.8\)) yields \(A(1^\circ)=(1.434\pm0.475)\times10^{-3}\), corresponding to \(\log_{10}A=-2.843^{+0.124}_{-0.175}\). We infer an effective large-scale bias by fitting \(Λ\)CDM projected-matter templates \(w_{\rm DM}(θ)\) computed with \textsc{CAMB} and Limber projection, including an integral-constraint correction evaluated from random--random weights. Using two bracketing T-RECS redshift-distribution priors, we obtain \(b_{\rm eff}=1.998\pm0.350\) (AGN prior) and \(b_{\rm eff}=1.530\pm0.265\) (TOTAL prior), demonstrating that the dominant modelling uncertainty arises from \(N(z)\). As a derived summary we Limber-invert the power-law amplitude to obtain \(r_0=6.18\pm1.13\) and \(5.59\pm1.02~h^{-1}\mathrm{Mpc}\) for the AGN and TOTAL priors, respectively. These results establish MeerKLASS UHF DR1 as a new wide-area, intermediate-frequency dataset for radio-continuum clustering. As MeerKLASS expands and overlapping optical/IR spectroscopy provides improved redshift calibration, future releases will enable population-split clustering and bias evolution measurements with substantially reduced modelling uncertainty.

</details>


### [120] [Radio spectral properties and aging of two tailed radio galaxies in a galaxy group at z=0.35](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22595)
*Paula Vulić,Vernesa Smolčić,Alexis Finoguenov,Ghassem Gozaliasl,Hiddo Sunnz Bouwe Algera,Ivan Delvecchio*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该研究分析了一个正在合并的年轻星系群核心中的两个尾射电星系（一个宽角尾WAT和一个头尾HT），利用多波段射电观测（VLA、MeerKAT、GMRT）研究其射电形态、谱特性及年龄。发现谱年龄（WAT:~34 Myr, HT:~21 Myr）远小于动力学年龄（WAT:420-700 Myr, HT:140-280 Myr），比值为~7-20，表明星系与IGM存在强烈相互作用和能量注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究星系群合并过程中射电星系与星系际介质的相互作用，并理解两者之间的能量反馈机制。

Method: 利用VLA（3 GHz, 1.4 GHz）、MeerKAT（1.35 GHz）和GMRT（610 MHz, 325 MHz）多频率射电观测，分析两个尾射电星系的形态、谱指数分布，并利用Jaffe-Perola模型计算谱年龄，通过与动力学年龄对比来评估活动时间尺度。

Result: WAT谱指数α=0.8±0.1，HT谱指数α=0.6±0.2；两者均呈现从核心向外谱指数增大的趋势，局部存在谱平坦化区域。谱年龄（WAT: 33.80+7.63/-7.23 Myr, HT: 20.86+10.07/-17.17 Myr）远小于动力学年龄（WAT: 420-700 Myr, HT: 140-280 Myr），比值约7-20。

Conclusion: 两个尾射电星系正在与IGM主动相互作用并注入能量；谱年龄与动力学年龄的巨大差异表明现有简单模型可能存在局限，未来需要更复杂的动力学模型、更深X射线观测以及高频宽带射电观测来进一步约束。

Abstract: We present a study of two tailed radio galaxies in the core of a massive, dynamically young galaxy group - an early group-group merger. Using VLA (3 GHz and 1.4 GHz), MeerKAT (1.35 GHz), and GMRT (610 and 325 MHz) observations, we investigate their radio spectral properties, spectral and dynamical ages. Radio morphologies show clear evidence of interaction with the intragroup medium (IGM). One galaxy is a wide-angle tail (WAT) source, while the other is most likely a head-tail (HT) galaxy. Both galaxies exhibit high radio luminosities, and we find spectral indices of $α=0.8\pm 0.1$ (WAT) and $α=0.6\pm 0.2$ (HT). Spectral index analysis reveals spectral steepening with distance from the core in both galaxies, with localized flattening in the WAT lobes and hotspots along the northern jet, and indications of such flattening in the middle of the HT tail. Spectral ages derived using Jaffe-Perola model are $33.80\substack{+7.63 \\ -7.23}$ Myr (WAT) and $20.86\substack{+10.07 \\ -17.17}$ Myr (HT), significantly lower than dynamical ages of $420\pm60$ to $700\pm100$ Myr (WAT) and $140\pm20$ Myr (and possibly up to $280\pm40$ Myr, for HT), yielding dynamical-to-spectral age ratios of $\sim12-20$ and $\sim7$ (and up to $\sim14$), respectively. The discrepancy may be reduced by using more complex dynamical age models, incorporating interactions with the IGM, which requires deeper X-ray observations of the group. Spectral age estimates may be affected by mixing of electron populations, and could be better constrained with future deep, high-resolution broad-band radio observations at both MHz and frequencies above 3 GHz. The combination of extended radio structures, spectral signatures of radiative aging with localized re-acceleration, and activity timescales up to hundreds of Myr indicates that galaxies are actively interacting with, and likely depositing energy into their environment.

</details>


### [121] [Understanding the non-Gaussian nature of Galactic foreground emissions towards small scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22596)
*Fazlu Rahman,Pravabati Chingangbam,Kevin Huffenberger,Tuhin Ghosh,Dmitri Pogosyan,Tarun Souradeep,Changbom Park*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用Minkowski泛函和广义偏度-峰度参数，对银河系前景辐射的非高斯性进行了统一的多尺度研究。发现所有前景成分均表现出以过量峰度为主导的普适非高斯特性，其形状在不同角尺度上保持稳定。通过对比观测图与尘埃模型实现，评估了现有模型在再现非高斯性质方面的表现，并揭示了普适峰度特征的物理起源来自重尾单点统计与陡峭大尺度空间相关的结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解银河系前景辐射的小尺度非高斯性及其物理起源，评估现有前景模型在再现观测非高斯性质方面的准确性，为未来CMB分析中前景小尺度建模提供验证框架。

Method: 采用Minkowski泛函和广义偏度-峰度参数进行统一的多尺度非高斯性分析；对比GNILC和Planck 545 GHz观测图与PySM及基于纤维的尘埃模型实现；利用基于PDF的简单玩具模型解释物理机制。

Result: 所有前景成分均表现出以过量峰度为主导的普适非高斯性，形状跨尺度稳定；GNILC与PySM在全局尺度上峰度行为匹配良好，但PySM在区域分析中存在显著空间变异性差异；纤维模型产生的偏度和峰度信号明显偏弱；普适峰度特征源于重尾分布与陡峭空间相关的结合。

Conclusion: 过量峰度是银河系前景辐射的稳健统计指纹，现有模型（如PySM）虽能再现整体非高斯幅度和尺度依赖性，但未能完全捕捉观测峰度信号的空间变异性，为未来CMB小尺度前景模型验证提供了实用框架。

Abstract: We present a unified, multi-scale study of non-Gaussianity of Galactic foreground emissions using Minkowski Functionals and generalized skewness-kurtosis parameters, focusing on the characterization of small-scale non-Gaussianity and its underlying physical origin. We find that all foreground components studied exhibit a remarkably universal non-Gaussian nature dominated by excess kurtosis, whose shape remains stable across angular scales despite large differences in emission physics. Focusing on thermal dust, we perform a detailed comparison between observed maps (GNILC and Planck 545 GHz) and dust model realizations (PySM and filament-based models) to assess the performance of state-of-the-art models in reproducing the observed non-Gaussian properties. At the global level, GNILC and PySM display closely matched kurtosis behavior over the angular scales where the GNILC reconstruction is reliable, while the filament-based model produces substantially weaker skewness and kurtosis signals. For PySM, however, a patch-based analysis reveals statistically significant regional variations, indicating that while the model reproduces the overall non-Gaussian amplitude and scale dependence, it does not fully capture the spatial variability of the observed kurtosis signal. Using simple PDF-based toy models, we demonstrate that the universal kurtosis signature arises from the combination of heavy-tailed one-point statistics and steep large-scale spatial correlations, while its detailed amplitude and scale dependence depend on the underlying foreground physics. These results identify excess kurtosis as a robust statistical fingerprint of Galactic foregrounds and provide a practical framework for validating small-scale foreground models for future CMB analyses.

</details>


### [122] [A cross-calibration approach for polarisation-sensitive detectors in CMB experiments: application to LiteBIRD's polarisation angle calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23207)
*A. Novelli,F. Piacentini,S. Micheli,E. Allys,A. Anand,J. Aumont,A. J. Banday,R. B. Barreiro,N. Bartolo,S. Basak,A. Basyrov,A. Besnard,D. Blinov,M. Bortolami,T. Brinckmann,F. Cacciotti,E. Calabrese,P. Campeti,A. Carones,F. Carralot,F. J. Casas,J. Chandran,K. Cheung,M. Citran,L. Clermont,F. Columbro,A. Coppolecchia,F. Cuttaia,P. de Bernardis,T. de Haan,E. de la Hoz,M. De Lucia,P. Diego-Palazuelos,H. K. Eriksen,F. Finelli,C. Franceschet,U. Fuskeland,G. Galloni,M. Galloway,M. Gerbino,M. Gervasi,R. T. Génova-Santos,T. Ghigna,S. Giardiello,C. Gimeno-Amo,A. Gruppuso,M. Hazumi,S. Henrot-Versillé,L. T. Hergt,E. Hivon,H. Ishino,K. Kohri,L. Lamagna,M. Lattanzi,C. Leloup,F. Levrier,A. I. Lonappan,M. López-Caniego,G. Luzzi,J. Macias-Perez,V. Maranchery,E. Martínez-González,S. Masi,S. Matarrese,T. Matsumura,M. Migliaccio,M. Monelli,G. Morgante,L. Mousset,R. Nagata,T. Namikawa,P. Natoli,F. Noviello,L. Pagano,A. Paiella,D. Paoletti,G. Pascual-Cisneros,G. Patanchon,G. Piccirilli,M. Pinchera,G. Polenta,L. Porcelli,M. Remazeilles,A. Ritacco,M. Ruiz-Granda,Y. Sakurai,L. Salvati,J. Sanghavi,V. Sauvage,D. Scott,M. Shiraishi,G. Signorelli,R. M. Sullivan,Y. Takase,L. Terenzi,M. Tomasi,M. Tristram,L. Vacher,B. van Tent,P. Vielva,S. Vinzl,I. K. Wehus,G. Weymann-Despres,E. J. Wollack,M. Zannoni*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于CMB实验探测器交叉校准的算法，通过迭代比较单探测器地图与频段平均地图来校准极化角度等参数。在LiteBIRD模拟中验证达到角分精度，引入的r偏置远低于系统预算(δr < 6.5×10⁻⁶)，适用于下一代CMB实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学观测中的一个挑战是获得高精度CMB极化图以测量原初B模并约束张量标量比r，该信号极其微弱，受到前景和E-to-B泄漏的干扰，需要大型探测器阵列和多频段精确校准。

Method: 提出了一种交叉校准算法，迭代比较单探测器地图与频段平均地图，可应用于任何能在地图层面观测和校正的校准参数，从而放宽发射前校准要求并实现后处理验证。

Result: 在模拟LiteBIRD观测中验证了极化角度校准，包括随机探测器失校准和晶圆级旋转，算法收敛到角分精度。通过参数化(FgBuster)和盲(HILC)成分分离及r估计管道传播残差校准不确定性，诱导的r偏置远低于LiteBIRD系统预算δr < 6.5×10⁻⁶。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于下一代CMB实验，能有效降低校准需求并保证系统误差在预算范围内。

Abstract: One of the current challenges in observational cosmology is obtaining high-precision polarisation maps of the CMB to measure primordial $B$-modes and constrain the tensor-to-scalar ratio ($r$). The weakness of this signal compared to foregrounds and $E$-to-$B$ leakage makes this task particularly challenging, requiring large detector arrays operating at multiple frequencies and extremely precise calibration. We present a cross-calibration algorithm to determine relative calibration of detectors within the same frequency band of a CMB experiment. The method iteratively compares single-detector maps with band-averaged maps and can be applied to any calibration parameter that can be observed and corrected at the map level, relaxing pre-flight calibration requirements and enabling post-processing validation. We validate the pipeline by calibrating the polarisation angle of simulated LiteBIRD observations, including both random detector miscalibration and wafer-level rotations. The algorithm converges to correct values with arcminute precision. Finally, we propagate residual calibration uncertainties through component separation and tensor-to-scalar ratio estimation pipelines using both parametric (FgBuster) and blind (HILC) methods. The induced bias on $r$ remains well below the LiteBIRD systematics budget of $δr < 6.5\times10^{-6}$, demonstrating that the method is suitable for next-generation CMB experiments.

</details>


### [123] [Wedge-avoidance Fisher Forecasts for Primordial Non-Gaussianity from Dark-Ages 21-cm Power Spectrum and Bispectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23314)
*Chen Zhang,Furen Deng,Yidong Xu,Bin Yue,Yuewei Wen,Xuelei Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种考虑前景楔形规避效应的Fisher矩阵框架，用于预测21厘米信号对原初非高斯性的约束能力。以月球背面阵列的热噪声为例，分析共振模型和阶梯模型两种暴胀模型中的振荡特征，发现楔形规避显著减少了可用傅里叶模式，大幅削弱了对原初非高斯性振幅的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑暗时代的高红移21厘米信号是探测原初非高斯性的理想窗口，但干涉测量中的前景楔形规避会移除大量傅里叶模式，尤其在高红移时更为严重。现有预测方法未充分考虑这一效应，需要开发能直接传播模式损失的框架。

Method: 提出一种楔形感知的Fisher框架，将前景楔形规避导致的模式损失直接传播到柱坐标21厘米功率谱和约化双谱的方差中。以月球背面阵列为案例，在包含热噪声的条件下，对共振模型和阶梯模型两种暴胀模型中的振荡特征进行约束力预测，并比较功率谱与双谱的约束能力。

Result: 楔形规避显著减少了功率谱和双谱中的可用傅里叶模式，导致对原初非高斯性振幅的约束大幅减弱。两种振荡特征模型下的约束力均明显下降。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架具有广泛适用性，可用于不同红移下的21厘米功率谱和双谱预测，尤其适用于前景楔形效应严重的时期（如再电离纪元和高红移黑暗时代）。

Abstract: The Dark Ages offer a uniquely clean window on primordial physics, making the redshifted 21-cm signal a promising probe of primordial non-Gaussianity (PNG). Forecasts for interferometric 21-cm measurements must account for foreground wedge avoidance, which removes a substantial fraction of Fourier modes, and this effect is especially severe at high redshifts. We develop a wedge-aware Fisher framework in which this mode loss is propagated directly into the variances of the cylindrical 21-cm power spectrum and reduced bispectrum. As a case study, we apply the method to forecast PNG constraints in two inflation models with different oscillatory features, a resonant model and a step model, for a lunar far-side array including thermal noise. Since these features affect both the power spectrum and the bispectrum, we apply wedge avoidance to both forecasts and compare their constraining power on PNG amplitudes in these oscillatory feature models. We find that wedge avoidance reduces available Fourier modes significantly in both observables, leading to much weaker constraints on PNG. This framework is broadly applicable to 21-cm power spectrum and bispectrum forecasts across redshifts and is particularly useful in regimes where foreground wedge effect is severe, such as the epoch of reionization and the higher-redshift Dark Ages.

</details>


### [124] [Field-level weak lensing cosmology with $<100$ simulations using multifidelity simulation-based inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23346)
*Alex A. Saoulis,Kiyam Lin,Niall Jeffrey,Maximilian von Wietersheim-Kramsta,Davide Piras,Alessio Spurio Mancini,Ana M. G. Ferreira,Benjamin Joachimi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出多保真度模拟推断方法，利用不到100个N体模拟实现KiDS-Legacy巡天层面的弱引力透镜场压缩与推断，相较于传统两点统计方法可大幅节省高保真模拟成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场级推断能充分利用弱透镜剪切场中的宇宙学信息，但物理真实性要求极高保真模拟，导致传统模拟推断（SBI）需要大量昂贵的高保真训练模拟，成本过高。

Method: 采用多保真度SBI策略：先用快速log-normal GLASS模拟预训练神经推断模型，再在少量（60-100个）高保真N体模拟上微调，实现场级神经压缩与推断。

Result: 60-100个高保真模拟即可获得信息丰富且校准良好的宇宙学后验分布，将场级推断的模拟成本降低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 多保真度SBI有效缓解了场级推断中物理真实性与计算成本之间的权衡，在实际巡天分析中具有显著可行性。

Abstract: We perform a realistic KiDS-Legacy mock analysis with field-level neural compression and simulation-based inference using fewer than 100 $N$-body simulations. The weak lensing shear field encodes substantially more cosmological information than standard two-point summary statistics such as the power spectrum. Field-level inference can fully exploit this information, but physical realism at the field-level requires very high-fidelity simulations. This poses a major challenge for simulation-based inference (SBI): accurate empirical density modelling and deep-learning-based neural compression require many training simulations, but achieving physical realism at the field level makes each simulation extremely costly. We demonstrate that multifidelity SBI can alleviate this tension by substantially reducing the number of high-fidelity simulations needed for accurate cosmological inference. We pre-train neural inference models on realistic KiDS-Legacy-like shear mocks using fast log-normal GLASS simulations and fine-tune them on a small set of high-fidelity $N$-body simulations. We show that between $60$-$100$ high-fidelity simulations are sufficient to obtain informative and well-calibrated cosmological posteriors, enabling an order-of-magnitude reduction in simulation cost for accurate field-level inference in a realistic setting.

</details>


### [125] [DSWIM:Efficient and Stable Deterministic Computation of Warm Inflation Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23518)
*Umang Kumar*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出DSWIM，一种基于相关矩阵演化的确定性模块，通过物理启发的缩放矩阵变换改善热膨胀扰动计算中的数值条件，在保持精度的同时显著提升计算效率并消除数值伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热膨胀扰动的随机实现计算成本高，而基于相关矩阵演化的确定性方法虽高效但存在数值病态问题——扰动变量在不同尺度上演化时，数值条件不佳会影响稳定性和准确性。

Method: 在SWIM代码基础上引入确定性模块DSWIM，提出从扰动变量的有效哈勃缩放推导的物理启发式缩放矩阵变换，该变换精确保持原初曲率功率谱的同时改善数值条件；通过扩散矩阵结构自然处理相关热噪声贡献。

Result: 代表性热膨胀模型测试表明：缩放框架抑制了数值伪影，增强了确定性演化的鲁棒性，在保持精度前提下显著提升计算速度；扩散矩阵结构自然引入相关热噪声，解决了此前随机与确定性实现之间的不一致性。

Conclusion: DSWIM为计算热膨胀标量扰动提供了数值稳健且计算高效的框架，可作为随机方法的有效替代或补充工具。

Abstract: Warm inflation perturbations are sourced by both thermal and quantum fluctuations and are commonly computed through stochastic realizations of the perturbation equations, as implemented in the publicly available code SWIM. Deterministic formulations based on correlation matrix evolution provide a computationally efficient alternative, but can become numerically ill-conditioned when the perturbation variables evolve over widely different scales. In this work, we extend SWIM by introducing a deterministic module, DSWIM, based on correlation matrix evolution. We introduce a physically motivated scaling matrix transformation derived from the effective Hubble scaling of the perturbation variables. The transformed system preserves the primordial curvature power spectrum exactly while substantially improving the numerical conditioning of the deterministic evolution equations. Using representative warm inflation models, we show that the scaled framework suppresses numerical artifacts, improves the robustness of the deterministic evolution, and yields substantial computational speedups while preserving accuracy. We further show that correlated thermal noise contributions arise naturally through the diffusion matrix structure, resolving previously observed discrepancies between stochastic and deterministic implementations. Our results establish DSWIM as a numerically robust and computationally efficient framework for computing warm inflation scalar perturbations.

</details>


### [126] [Unifying the Dark Sector with the New Generalized Chaplygin Gas: Observational Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23563)
*Abdulla Al Mamon,Andronikos Paliathanasis,Subhajit Saha*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种红移依赖指数的广义Chaplygin气体模型，将其作为统一描述暗能量和暗物质的框架，利用Ia型超新星、宇宙计时器和DESI BAO数据进行贝叶斯分析，发现该模型在似然值上优于ΛCDM，但考虑参数数量后两者在统计上不可区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索广义Chaplygin气体模型能否作为统一描述暗能量和暗物质的可行框架，并利用最新宇宙学观测数据对其进行检验。

Method: 采用红移依赖指数的广义Chaplygin气体模型，将其作为单一统一流体成分；使用Ia型超新星、宇宙计时器和DESI第二批数据中的重子声波振荡数据；进行贝叶斯分析估计参数，并与ΛCDM模型比较。

Result: 广义Chaplygin气体模型给出的组合似然值系统性高于ΛCDM；但考虑更多自由参数后，贝叶斯证据和Akaike信息准则表明该模型与ΛCDM在统计上不可区分。

Conclusion: 广义Chaplygin气体模型与ΛCDM模型在统计上无显著差异，当前数据不足以偏好其中任一模型。

Abstract: In light of recent cosmological observations, we examine a generalized Chaplygin gas model with a redshift-dependent exponent as a framework for describing the dark energy and dark matter content of the Universe. Specifically, we treat this fluid as a single unified component and test it against late-time background observational data. We employ Type Ia supernova data, cosmic chronometers, and baryon acoustic oscillations from the second data release of the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument. We perform a Bayesian analysis for parameter estimation and compare the model with $Λ$CDM. We find that the generalized Chaplygin gas provides systematically higher values of the combined likelihood; nevertheless, once the larger number of free parameters is taken into account, both the Bayesian evidence and the Akaike Information Criterion suggest that the model is statistically indistinguishable from $Λ$CDM.

</details>


### [127] [Revisiting the 'Lensing is Low' Problem with UNIONS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23651)
*Martine C. T. Campbell,Jack Elvin-Poole,Michael J. Hudson,Thomas de Boer,Sacha Guerrini,Fabian Hervas-Peters,Hendrik Hildebrandt,Martin Kilbinger,Eugene A. Magnier,Alan W. McConnachie,Charlie T. Mpetha,Romain Paviot,Ludovic Van Waerbeke,Anna Wittje*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用UNIONS巡天数据对BOSS CMASS星系的星系-星系引力透镜（GGL）信号进行新测量，联合建模GGL与星系成团性（GC）信号，发现此前报道的“透镜信号偏低”问题在CMASS样本中并不显著，最佳联合拟合仅需略微降低物质功率谱振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视“透镜信号偏低”问题——即基于CMB宇宙学参数、仅用GC数据标定的星系-晕连接模型会高估GGL信号20-40%，检验该效应在更大尺度数据下是否仍然成立。

Method: 利用UNIONS巡天约2650平方度重叠区域的高质量背景源成像，获取CMASS星系的精确大尺度GGL测量；采用晕占据分布（HOD）模型描述星系-晕连接，分别进行仅GC拟合以及GGL+GC联合拟合。

Result: 未发现显著的“透镜信号偏低”效应；最佳联合拟合需将物质功率谱振幅相对于Planck宇宙学参数略微降低；两个模型（HOD+宇宙学参数自由；HOD+宇宙学+反馈参数自由）均能同样好地描述观测数据。

Conclusion: 大尺度数据对约束透镜信号偏低效应至关重要，应将讨论从仅聚焦小尺度转向包含大尺度的整体视角。

Abstract: We present new measurements of the galaxy-galaxy lensing (GGL) signal around Baryon Oscillation Spectroscopic Survey (BOSS) CMASS galaxies using background sources from the Ultraviolet Near-Infrared Optical Northern Survey (UNIONS). With high-quality imaging of background sources and a survey overlap of approximately 2650 square degrees, we obtain precise large-scale GGL measurements. Building on these new measurements, we revisit the so-called 'lensing is low' problem, wherein galaxy-halo connection models calibrated on galaxy clustering (GC) data over-predict the GGL signal by 20-40% assuming CMB-based cosmological parameters. We model the galaxy-halo connection using a halo occupation distribution (HOD), and perform joint fits to both GGL and GC signals across a wide range of scales, as well as a GC-only fit. In contrast to previous work, we do not find a significant 'lensing is low' effect in the CMASS sample, although the best joint fits are achieved by decreasing the amplitude of the matter power spectrum slightly relative to the Planck cosmological parameters. Overall, we find that two models describe our observables similarly well: one where HOD and cosmological parameters are free, and one where HOD, cosmological, and feedback parameters are free. Importantly, we emphasise the role of large scales in constraining the lensing is low effect, shifting the narrative away from an exclusively small-scale issue.

</details>

### [128] [Relational Gaze Transitions During Encoding Predict Episodic Recall of Naturalistic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20844)
*Hugo Rydel,Alex Kafkas*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究通过场景图标注与眼动追踪技术，发现人们在自然场景观看和空白屏幕回忆时，目光会以高于随机水平的方式在语义相关的物体间移动（关系性扫描），且编码阶段的关系性扫描可预测后续对物体和关系的记忆表现，而检索阶段的扫描则无此效应，表明关系性目光是情景记忆编码的行为标志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视觉场景记忆如何将分散的细节组织成连贯事件，以及关系引导的目光转移是否能作为这种认知组织的行为标志。

Method: 利用场景图标注对眼动数据进行标注，测量复杂自然场景中目光是否在语义相关的物体间移动，量化自然场景中的关系性扫描行为。

Result: 1) 在初始观看和空白屏幕检索阶段，被试的关系性目光均高于随机水平；2) 编码阶段的关系性扫描可预测后续物体和关系的自由回忆成绩（控制显著性、注视频率、语义和图像差异后仍显著）；3) 检索阶段的关系性扫描不能预测回忆成功。

Conclusion: 关系性目光可在复杂场景中测量，并可能作为自然视觉探索中情景编码的行为标志。

Abstract: Remembering a visual scene requires organizing distinct details into a cohesive event. This study investigates whether relation-guided gaze transitions provide a behavioural marker of this cognitive organization during episodic encoding and retrieval. By applying scene graph annotations to eye-tracking data, we measured whether gaze moved between objects that were meaningfully related within complex scenes. This approach allowed us to quantify relational scanning within naturalistic environments, moving beyond prior methods that relied on simplified displays or isolated relation types. Participants showed above-chance relational gaze during both initial viewing and blank-screen retrieval, indicating that gaze actively tracks scene structure during first viewing and at recall. Additionally, relational scanning at encoding predicted subsequent free recall of both object and relational details, even after accounting for salience, fixation frequency, meaning, and image-level differences. In contrast, relational scanning at retrieval did not predict recall success, suggesting that relational gaze is most functional to memory during its formation. Together, these findings show that relational gaze can be measured in complex scenes and may serve as a marker of episodic encoding during natural visual exploration.

</details>


### [129] [Adaptive conduction delays and phase locking in spiking Haken Lighthouse networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21508)
*Stephen Coombes,Rüdiger Thul,Stefan Ruschel,Rachel Nicks*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文利用Haken Lighthouse模型理论分析了延迟尖峰网络中的锁相活动，推导了固定延迟下的自洽条件和线性稳定性理论，并引入活动依赖性白质可塑性规则（髓鞘调节轴突传导速度），揭示了自适应延迟如何重塑网络吸引子结构并产生长时间尺度转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解延迟尖峰网络中的锁相活动提供解析可处理的数学框架，并探究活动依赖性髓鞘化如何调节时间协调、同步性和通过相干性实现通信。

Method: 基于Haken Lighthouse事件驱动模型，推导锁相态自洽条件和尖峰时间扰动的线性稳定性理论；引入活动依赖白质可塑性规则，构建带状态依赖延迟的慢-快系统，利用循环对称性进行傅里叶模态分解分析。

Result: 可塑性规则选择共度延迟-周期关系，实现同步、频率锁定、锁相模式间的慢速切换以及异质延迟组织为离散延迟-周期类别；事件驱动模拟验证了解析预测。

Conclusion: 该框架为研究活动依赖性髓鞘化如何调节尖峰神经系统的时间协调、同步性和通信相干性提供了可处理的数学工具。

Abstract: We develop a theory of phase-locked activity in delayed spiking networks using the Haken Lighthouse model as an analytically tractable event-based description of neural dynamics. For networks with fixed delays, we derive self-consistency conditions for phase-locked states and an associated linear stability theory formulated directly in terms of spike-time perturbations. The framework is illustrated for a delayed autapse, a reciprocally coupled two-cell network, and spatially structured rings with distance-dependent coupling and conduction delays, where circulant symmetry allows stability to be decomposed into Fourier modes. We then introduce an activity-dependent white matter plasticity rule in which myelination modulates axonal conduction speed and hence communication delay. This leads naturally to a slow--fast system with state-dependent delays, in which frozen phase-locked branches organise the adaptive dynamics. The plasticity rule selects commensurate delay--period relationships, providing a mechanism for the emergence of synchrony, other frequency-locked states, slow switching between competing phase-locked patterns, and the organisation of heterogeneous delays into discrete delay--period classes. Direct simulations of the event-driven network support the analytical predictions and illustrate how adaptive conduction can reshape the attractor structure of a delayed spiking network and generate long-timescale transitions. These results provide a tractable mathematical framework for studying how activity-dependent myelination may regulate temporal coordination, synchrony, and communication through coherence in spiking neural systems.

</details>


### [130] [Delay coordinates synchronization and induces abrupt transition in excitable networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21703)
*Bruno R. R. Boaretto,Kalel L. Rossi,Lyle E. Muller,Elbert E. Macau,Roberto C. Budzinski*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 兴奋性神经元连接中的时间延迟可通过产生自持振荡（异相或同相）协调同步模式，并因连接强度或延迟的微小变化引发向同相同步的突然爆炸性转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经元通信中不可避免的时间延迟如何从看似干扰的因素转变为赋予神经电路有用功能的机制，特别是延迟如何协调神经元的同步模式。

Method: 通过理论分析揭示延迟兴奋性连接中神经元可激动力学与信号传输延迟的相互作用机制，并在不同网络连接性、神经元模型、有无兴奋性及噪声条件下验证该现象的普适性。

Result: 延迟导致自持振荡（异相或同相）的出现，并引发连接强度或时间延迟微小变化下向同相同步的爆炸性（突发性）转变。

Conclusion: 延迟在神经电路中不仅是挑战，还可以通过可激动力学与延迟的相互作用产生有用的同步调控功能，且该机制具有高度普适性。

Abstract: Neuronal communication is inherently time-delayed, due to the finite speed of signal propagation. Although often considered challenging or disruptive, such time delays can also endow neural circuits with useful capabilities. Here, we show that delays in excitatory connections between excitable neurons coordinate their synchronization patterns by creating self-sustained oscillations that may be out-of-phase or in-phase. The emergence of these oscillations leads to an abrupt, explosive, transition to in-phase synchronized regimes due to small changes in connection strength or time-delay. We describe the mechanism underlying these phenomena as an interaction between the neuron's excitable dynamics and the delay in signal transmission, explaining many aspects of how the oscillations emerge. We show this phenomenon in different network connectivities, neuronal models, with and without excitation, with and without noise, highlighting the generality of the mechanism.

</details>


### [131] [Mostly-monocular responses and other visual functions in a multiscale network model of Macaque V1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21785)
*Zhuo-Cheng Xiao,Kevin K. Lin,Lai-Sang Young*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文通过猕猴V1层4Cα的计算模型，研究双眼视觉信号在初级视觉皮层的首个整合阶段。发现：沿眼优势柱边界出现窄双眼带；边界处10-30%的交互为跨柱交互；第6层反馈主要是单眼的。该多尺度模型比生物细节模型快数个数量级，可有效桥接解剖与功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究双眼视觉信号在初级视觉皮层（V1）沿大细胞通路、在4Cα层进行整合的神经解剖学起源，特别是该层中双眼反应的来源机制。

Method: 构建猕猴V1层4Cα的多尺度计算模型，通过系统假设检验来模拟双眼整合过程，模型速度比传统生物细节模型快数个数量级。

Result: (1) 眼优势柱边界处出现窄双眼带，与实验观察一致；(2) ODC边界处10-30%的交互为跨柱交互时与数据最吻合；(3) 第6层反馈主要为单眼性质。

Conclusion: 多尺度建模可作为连接神经解剖与功能的有效工具，有助于阐明视觉信号整合的机制。

Abstract: Visual signals from the two eyes merge gradually as they pass through the primary visual cortex (V1). Here we use a computational model of Macaque V1 to study the first stage of this integration \ktext{along the magnocellular pathway}, in layer 4C$α$, aiming to infer neuroanatomical origins of binocular response. \ktext{It is known that neurons in layer 4C$α$ are predominantly monocular, \ytext{though} some do exhibit varying degrees of binocularity. We find (1) the emergence of narrow binocular strips along borders of ocular dominance columns (ODC), a finding that aligns with experiments; (2) most consistent with data is when $10-30\%$ of interactions near ODC boundaries are cross-columnar; and (3) feedback from layer 6 is largely monocular.} These results were obtained through systematic hypothesis testing using a multiscale model that is orders of magnitude faster than its biologically-detailed predecessors. We propose that multiscale modeling can be an effective tool for bridging anatomy and function.

</details>


### [132] [Dynamic Computerized Tumbling-E Testing for Temporal Reliability of Human Sequential Perceptual Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21818)
*Avneek Sandhu,Bin Hu*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文通过计算机化的tumbling-E任务收集了人类试次级数据，将视觉敏锐度测试转化为具有时间分辨率的感知决策基准数据集，建立了人类基线以供未来与人工智能代理比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉敏锐度测试通常被视为静态阈值测量，但序贯感知决策随时间展开。计算机化的tumbling-E任务保留了响应延迟、超时和刺激大小自适应，创建了时间可靠性数据集而非仅图表分数。这对人-AI比较很重要，因为时间幻觉指数（THI）显示静态准确性可能掩盖延迟、漂移、持久性和不稳定收敛。

Method: 收集了来自计算机化动态tumbling-E任务的人类试次级数据。每个试次中，单个E视标出现在四个方向之一，参与者选择感知方向或超时，刺激大小通过自适应阶梯自动调整。主要指标包括反应时间、超时率、超过3秒预算的延迟率以及基于延迟和超时分量的THI。

Result: 最终数据集包含来自21名人类参与者在77次会话中的1,154个有效试次。其中1,078个非超时响应和76个超时响应，超时率为6.6%。非超时反应时间中位数约1.5秒（均值1546 ms；中位数1506 ms；IQR 1306-1713 ms），仅3个响应超过3000 ms。自适应中较小刺激转换占主导（89.2%）。平均弧分从试次0的29.42下降到试次19的5.04，支持收敛到接近20/20水平的视标。

Conclusion: 该数据集将tumbling-E视觉任务转化为时间解析的人类感知决策基准。其新颖贡献在于自动捕获阶梯行为、响应时间、超时和试次级可靠性信号。人类数据显示快速时间和平滑自适应向阈值收敛，建立了未来与人工智能代理比较的人类基线。

Abstract: OBJECTIVES: Visual acuity and tumbling-E tasks are often treated as static threshold measures, yet sequential perceptual decisions unfold over time. A computerized tumbling-E task preserves response latency, timeouts, and stimulus-size adaptation, creating a temporal reliability dataset rather than only a chart-line score. This matters for human-AI comparison because the Temporal Hallucination Index (THI) shows how static accuracy can obscure delays, drift, persistence, and unstable convergence. METHODS: We curated trial-level human data from a computerized dynamic tumbling-E task. On each trial, a single E optotype appeared in one of four orientations, participants selected the perceived direction or timed out, and stimulus size was automatically adjusted through an adaptive staircase. Primary outcomes were reaction time, timeout rate, delay rate above a 3-second budget, and observable THI based on delay and timeout components. RESULTS: The final dataset included 1,154 valid trials from 21 human identifiers across 77 sessions. There were 1,078 non-timeout responses and 76 timeouts, giving a 6.6% timeout rate. Non-timeout reaction times centered near 1.5 seconds (mean 1546 ms; median 1506 ms; IQR 1306-1713 ms), with only 3 responses exceeding 3,000 ms. Adaptation was dominated by smaller-next-stimulus transitions (89.2%). Mean arcminutes declined from 29.42 at trial 0 to 5.04 at trial 19, supporting convergence near a 20/20-level optotype without clinical acuity diagnosis. CONCLUSIONS: This dataset converts a tumbling-E visual task into a temporally resolved human perceptual-decision benchmark. Its novel contribution is automatic capture of staircase behavior, response timing, timeouts, and trial-level reliability signals. The human data show fast timing and smooth adaptation toward threshold, establishing a human-only baseline for future comparison with artificial agents.

</details>


### [133] [SPIDER -- Stitched Power-spectra for Inferring Directed information flow from incomplete and asynchronous Experimental Recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22695)
*Yisi S. Zhang,Daniel Y. Takahashi*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: SPIDER是一个非参数频域框架，能从非同步、不完整的多脑区记录中恢复有向信息流，通过拼接局部谱估计、核范数补全和规范谱分解实现无需时间对齐的有效连接分析，并在多物种、多模态数据上验证了其有效性，揭示了theta频段的跨物种层级化信息流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有向连接方法（Granger因果、DCM、PDC等）要求所有脑区同时记录且共享同一时钟，但大规模记录通常只采样部分脑区，不同实验、动物、实验室覆盖不同且有部分重叠的区域，缺乏共同时间参考，导致无法进行全脑有效连接分析。

Method: SPIDER是一个非参数频域框架：从重叠通道子集的局部功率谱估计拼接成全局谱矩阵，利用核范数补全填充从未同时观测的区域对，再通过规范谱分解和PDC获得频率分辨的有向交互，无需时间对齐。

Result: 在模拟、双光子钙成像和IBL Neuropixels数据集上验证，从12个实验室43个session中恢复了50个脑区间的有向信息流。发现theta频段存在显著的层级化前馈连接，海马结构为源。并在人类颅内EEG（43名患者，非重叠覆盖）中复现了相同的theta层级结构，跨物种、跨模态一致。

Conclusion: SPIDER使得此前无法进行有向流推断的多session、多动物数据集的全局脑有效连接分析变得可行。

Abstract: Mapping the directed flow of information between brain regions -- their effective connectivity -- is central to understanding brain function, yet large-scale recordings sample only a fraction of the brain at a time: sessions, animals, and laboratories cover different, partially overlapping regions, usually without a shared temporal reference. Established directed-connectivity methods (Granger causality, dynamic causal modeling, partial directed coherence, PDC) require all regions to be recorded simultaneously and with a common clock. We introduce SPIDER, a non-parametric, frequency-domain framework that recovers directed information flow from such incomplete, asynchronous recordings: it stitches local power-spectral estimates from overlapping channel subsets into a global spectral matrix and obtains frequency-resolved directed interactions by canonical spectral factorization and PDC, without temporal alignment, while nuclear-norm completion fills in never-co-observed region pairs. With consistency guarantees, we validate SPIDER on simulations, two-photon calcium imaging, and the International Brain Laboratory Neuropixels dataset, recovering directed flow among 50 areas from 43 sessions in 12 laboratories never recorded together. Beyond validation, SPIDER reveals what no single recording can: brain-wide spontaneous flow is largely recurrent, but in the theta band it forms a significant feedforward hierarchy with the hippocampal formation at its source. Applied to resting human intracranial EEG (43 patients, non-overlapping coverage), it recovers the same theta-band hierarchy across species and modality. SPIDER makes whole-brain effective-connectivity analysis tractable for multi-session, multi-animal datasets previously incompatible with directed-flow inference.

</details>


### [134] [Estimating common synaptic inputs to spinal motor neurons from motor unit spike trains using openhdemg](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23066)
*Helio V. Cabral,Giacomo Valli,Roberto Zanotti,Ioannis Delis,Francesco Negro*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提供了一份使用openhdemg开源框架从运动单位脉冲序列估计共同突触输入的实用指南，系统梳理了时域、频域和网络信息三类方法，给出了生理学解释、参数选择建议和完整工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前存在多种估计共同突触输入的分析方法，各自基于不同的生理假设和参数选择，但缺乏实用指南和开源实现，限制了可及性和可重复性。

Method: 将现有方法分为三类：用于平滑放电率的时域方法、基于累积脉冲序列间相干性的频域方法、以及基于非线性成对依赖和图论的网络信息方法。对每类方法提供生理解释、逐步估计过程、参数影响分析及选择建议，并给出从HDsEMG分解到共同突触输入估计的完整工作流程。

Result: 系统分析了参数选择对共同突触输入估计的影响，提供了实践建议；证明了分解质量直接影响估计结果。

Conclusion: 提供了一个开放、可复现且生理学基础扎实的框架，提升了共同突触输入估计的可及性和标准化水平。

Abstract: Common synaptic input is considered a fundamental principle of motor neuron control and represents the dominant component of the neural drive transmitted from the motor neurons to muscle. Recent advances in High-Density surface Electromyography (HDsEMG) and motor unit (MU) decomposition algorithms have enabled the concurrent identification of increasingly large populations of MUs and substantially expanded the possibility of estimating common synaptic input from MU spike trains, making this approach widely used to investigate the neural control of movement in humans. However, multiple analytical approaches are currently available, each relying on different physiological assumptions, mathematical formulations, and parameter choices. The lack of practical guidelines and open-source implementations has also limited the accessibility and reproducibility of these analyses. In this tutorial, we provide a practical, physiologically grounded guide to estimating common synaptic input from populations of MU spike trains using openhdemg, an open-source Python framework. We organize the available methods into three complementary categories: time-domain approaches applied to smoothed discharge rates, frequency-domain approaches based on coherence between cumulative spike trains, and a network-information approach based on nonlinear pairwise dependencies and graph theory. For each method, we describe its physiological interpretation, step-by-step estimation, and systematically examine how key parameter choices influence the resulting estimates, providing practical recommendations for their selection. Finally, we present a complete workflow from HDsEMG decomposition and MU cleaning to common synaptic input estimation, demonstrating that decomposition quality directly affects these estimates.

</details>


### [135] [The adaptive nature of confirmation bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23325)
*Dorje C. Brody,Karl J. Friston,Bernhard K. Meister,Emmanuel M. Pothos*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文在平方根概率空间（量子概率结构）上重新定义了确认偏误现象。通过将观测建模为矩阵而非随机变量，证明了在二元假设检验中最小化期望误差概率的最优证据选择会自然导致确认偏误，揭示了理性决策中包含确认偏误的惊人一面。顺序证据采样中，这种隐含最优性带来两个进化优势：最小记忆需求和误差概率随样本量指数级下降。主动推理框架下的最优证据与误差最小化结果一致，为主动量子推理提供了易于实现的协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上确认偏误被视为认知偏差，作者试图从量子概率框架出发，重新审视这一现象，探索其是否可能具有理性的基础。

Method: 采用平方根概率空间（量子概率结构）建模，将观测表示为矩阵而非随机变量；在二元假设检验中通过最小化期望误差概率寻找最优证据，并与主动推理框架下的信息最大化方法进行对比。

Result: 1) 最小化误差概率的最优证据选择会产生确认偏误；2) 顺序采样中这种最优性实现了最小记忆需求和误差概率指数级下降；3) 主动推理框架下的最优证据与误差最小化方法结果一致。

Conclusion: 确认偏误在量子概率框架下可被理解为理性决策的产物，而非纯粹的非理性偏差，为设计高效推理协议提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, the phenomenon generally classified as confirmation bias is formulated on the space of square-root probabilities (or equivalently, using the structures of quantum probability). In this framework, observations are modelled by matrices, rather than random variables on a probability space. In the problem of binary hypothesis testing, an optimal evidence choice minimises the expected error probability. We show that the resulting optimal choice of evidence leads to a confirmation bias, thus revealing a surprising aspect of rationality that encompasses confirmation bias. Specifically, in sequential evidence sampling, the implicit optimality leads to two remarkable evolutionary advantages, namely, (a) the decision maker requires only the smallest memory capacity, and (b) the error probability can be reduced exponentially in sample size. A complementary approach based on the framework of active inference -- where the decision maker seeks evidence that provides maximum information -- is then considered. The resulting optimal evidence is shown to agree with the one obtained by minimising error probability. Our framework provides an easy-to-implement protocol for an active quantum inference, whereby the optimal evidence choice for making an inference is sought over the space of matrices.

</details>
